# Library catalogue

Generated by `python tools/libfmt.py --catalogue`; do not edit by hand.
Every entry, who introduced it, and the worlds that reach it (through their
biome list, strata, chronicle events and structure slots). An entry no world
reaches is listed as unused, and one reached only by worlds kept out of
the repository as "no shipped world".

| ? | fae_mire | - | unused | The pit in an overgrown arena: still water gone luminous and sour, bright enough to light the walls above it and poisonous enough to kill anything that wades in. Close to the moss in colour and all but flat, so that what marks it out from the grass at the edge is the light coming off it rather than a pattern. Twelve damage a second, the same as the slag it stands in for, so a hazard the map was built around is still a hazard after the greening. |

| ? | ice_panel | transmission | porch, starfall, transmission | Wall panelling in a frozen base: off-white composite in metre courses, a little blue in the joints, rime in the seams. The bright surface that makes the place read as built rather than dug. |
| ? | ice_panel_amber | transmission | transmission | Composite wall panelling in a frozen base, painted signal amber: the comms and array rooms, warm against the ice. |
| ? | ice_panel_dark | transmission | transmission | The slate-blue trim and service panels of a frozen base: door frames, plinths, the backs of the galleries. Dark enough that a white wall beside it keeps its edge. |
| ? | ice_panel_mint | transmission | starfall, transmission | Composite wall panelling in a frozen base, a pale laboratory green: the cryo-labs, clean and a little clinical. |
| ? | ice_panel_rust | transmission | starfall, transmission | Composite wall panelling in a frozen base, painted oxide red: armouries and airlocks, the rooms that keep the weapons. |
| ? | ice_panel_teal | transmission | starfall, transmission | Composite wall panelling in a frozen base, painted the deep teal of the coolant plant: the colour of a base's core rooms. |
| ? | ice_panel_violet | transmission | starfall, transmission | Composite wall panelling in a frozen base, a cold violet: the uplink and transmitter bays, high and bright. |
| ? | ice_track | kestrel | unused | A racing surface of packed, scraped ice: blue-grey and darker than the snow beside it, faintly streaked, and gripping like rock under a kart (surface rock), unlike the snow. |
| ? | ice_wall | transmission | porch, starfall, transmission | The glacier a frozen base is cut into, where it is left as wall: blue-grey ice with darker blue in the blotches. Darker than glacier_ice, which is written for a fjord under open sky; indoors that one glares and every corner disappears. |
| ? | icicle | transmission | kaldvik, porch, starfall, transmission | An icicle hanging from a high ceiling. Passable, so a body runs through it rather than catching on it. |
| ? | jump_pad | arena | megalith, porch, starfall, transmission | The floor of a jump pad: a disc of warm amber light, so a pad that throws you can be seen from across the room. Painted by the arena tools over the floor under every kicker; it is floor, nothing more. |

| ? | kh_dogtown_spill | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | The pale subsoil a prairie dog kicks out of its burrow: dry, grey-buff, nothing growing on it. |

| ? | sea_palm | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | Sea palm: rubbery olive stipes with a mop of blades, standing on the rock where the surf hits. |
| ? | sea_star_ochre | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | Ochre sea stars, orange and purple, stuck to the rock at the waterline. |
| ? | shelf_fungus | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | Bracket fungus: plain cream, a shade darker here and there, on the shelves that stand out from old trunks. |
| ? | ship_hull | kaldvik | gannet, gannet_harbor, kaldvik | A wooden ship's hull, black with tar and greenheart sheathing, rimed white where the spray froze on it. |
| ? | ship_rigging | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, ribshore | Tarred spars and standing rigging, furred with hoar frost. |
| ? | ship_trim | kaldvik | gannet, gannet_harbor, kaldvik | The ship's rail and deckhouse trim, once white, now the colour of old ice. |
| ? | shore_hardpack | ribstrand | ribstrand | A racing road of beach sand rolled hard and damp over a bed of crushed shell: grey-brown, a shade darker than the dry dunes beside it and lighter than the wet beach, flecked pale with shell. Grips like hardpack. |
| ? | shore_kerb | ribstrand | ribstrand | Racing kerb for the grey coast, banded basalt black and bone white so the road's edge reads against both the dark wet sand and the pale dunes. |
| ? | shore_pine_needles | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand | Shore pine: short yellow-green needles in tufts at the branch ends. |
| ? | silver_deadwood | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Dead cypress and cedar weathered silver-grey, split along the grain: the ghost forest's snags, the bottle trees, the figure's cane. Drawn as thin stems (bevelled, not rounded), so slim branches stay joined. |
| ? | silver_leaves | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | Silver tree leaves: long and covered in silky hairs, so the whole crown flashes silver when the wind turns them. |
| ? | sinter | kaldvik | kaldvik, saurischia | Silica sinter round a hot spring: a pale grey-cream crust laid down in thin terraces, warm to stand on and never under snow. |
| ? | sinter_stain | kaldvik | kaldvik, saurischia | Sinter stained by the mats of algae and bacteria in the run-off: rust orange shading to olive where the water cools. |
| ? | sitka_needles | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, hemlow, kitehold, ribshore | Sitka spruce: stiff blue-green needles, silvery underneath. |
| ? | slag_pool | slagdeck | slagdeck | The pit at the middle of the deck: slag still hot enough to light the walls above it, and hot enough to kill anything that wades in. Twelve damage a second, so crossing a corner of it costs a little and standing in it is about eight seconds of life. |
| ? | sloes | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, windbarrow | Sloes: small blue-black plums with a pale bloom, autumn. |
| ? | sludge | slagdeck | bonebight, ctfbox, duelbox, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, megalith, peregrine, porch, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, slagdeck, sluice, starfall, thornpan, thornpan_delta, transmission, vermilion, windbarrow, zoo | What the goo gun leaves behind: a wet olive mass that clings where it lands, slumps when nothing holds it up, and is about to go off. It gives off a little light of its own, which is how you spot a doorway somebody has mined. Named in Rust (combat::sludge), so it is in worldload::ENGINE_MATERIALS and every world reaches it. |
| ? | spanish_moss | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Spanish moss: grey-green beards hanging from the oaks and cypress. Passable. |
| ? | sponge_orange | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | Barrel sponge: burnt orange, pocked. |
| ? | sponge_purple | underwater | bonebight, glassfall, jadewater | Tube sponges: deep purple, soft-looking. |
| ? | stall_green | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | Green-and-white striped canvas over a market stall. |
| ? | stall_red | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | Red-and-white striped canvas over a market stall. |
| ? | star_gold | kaldvik | gannet_harbor, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, saurischia | The star on the market spruce. |
| ? | steppe_grass | megalith | kitehold, kitehold_rock, megalith | Grazed chalk turf on an arena floor: short grass grazed and dried to tan, ankle-high and even, with the chalk showing through. Unlike the grasses of the downs it grows no stands of long grass (`src/mesh/sprites.rs` names it); its name holds `grass` so the straw (world.json `straw`) reaches it, so nothing on an arena floor is tall enough to hide in. |
| ? | stick_wood | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, vermilion, windbarrow | Peeled sticks lashed into lattices: grey, dry, a little twisted. |
| ? | stockfish | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | Cod split and hung in pairs to dry in the frost: grey-gold, stiff as boards. |
| ? | straw_bound | kaldvik | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kitehold, redrock, saurischia | Straw bound tight in bundles, golden and bristling. |
| ? | straw_stem | thornpan | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, kitehold_rock, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | Straw-green jointed stems: common reed canes, Mormon tea, rabbitbrush. Passable. Replaces reed_cane, ephedra_stem and rabbitbrush_stem. |
| ? | sunflower_head | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | Sunflowers (drawn as sprites): yellow heads over coarse green leaves. |
| ? | sunflower_star | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | A sunflower star's soft body, pink-orange and many-armed. |
| ? | swamp_muck | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Black-brown swamp mud under the cypress: wet, with a litter of rusty needles. |
| ? | switchcane_culm | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Switchcane culms: olive green, jointed. Passable: you push through a canebrake. |
| ? | tamarisk_bark | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Tamarisk stems: red-brown, thin and many. |
| ? | tamarisk_flowers | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Tamarisk flower plumes: soft pink. |
| ? | teleport_pad | arena | megalith, porch, starfall, transmission | The floor of a teleporter: a disc of violet light, so a pad that sends you somewhere can be seen from across the room. Painted by the arena tools over the floor under every teleporter; it is floor, nothing more. |
| ? | temple_cedar | bonebight | bonebight | Planed cedar for the temple's posts and walls: warm brown gone grey on the weather side. |
| ? | temple_floor | bonebight | bonebight | The temple's open wooden floors, polished by bare feet: honey brown. |
| ? | terracotta | underwater | bonebight, jadewater, lowmouth, saltmirror, saurischia, windbarrow | Unglazed red earthenware: storage jars, a little paler where the sea has worn them. |
| ? | thimbleberry_cane | glassfall | glassfall, redrock, vermilion | Thimbleberry canes: pale brown with shreddy bark. Passable. |
| ? | tile_slate | - | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | Blue-grey roofing slate. Smoothed, so a roof at an angle to the grid is a slope rather than a staircase. |
| ? | tile_terracotta | - | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | Orange clay roof tiles. Smoothed, so a roof at an angle to the grid is a slope rather than a staircase. |
| ? | titan_bone | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia | The titan's bone: dark wet grey, stained by the sea and the sand, paler where the rain runs. |
| ? | titan_eye | ribshore | ribshore | The light in the titan's eye: the lighthouse lantern's amber, faint, lit only at night. |

| ? | tomb_lime | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Whitewashed plaster over brick: the raised tombs, stained grey where the water stood. |
| ? | track_kerb | kestrel | peregrine | Red and white racing kerb along the edges of a circuit (event circuit's kerb). |
| ? | transporter_yellow | saltmirror | saltmirror | The transporter's paint: a highway-machine yellow faded to mustard, worn to rust along every edge. |
| ? | tree_fern_frond | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | Tree fern fronds: long arching fans of bright lacy green. |
| ? | tree_fern_trunk | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | A tree fern's trunk: a column of matted dark brown root fibre and old frond bases. |

| ? | trillium | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold | Trillium (drawn as sprites): three broad leaves under one white three-petalled flower. |
| ? | truck_cream | saltmirror | saltmirror | Two-tone cream on a 1970s pickup, gone the colour of old teeth, rust along the sills. |
| ? | truck_orange | saltmirror | gannet, gannet_harbor, saltmirror | Harvest orange on a 1970s pickup, faded pale, rust coming through the bonnet. |

| ? | try_brick | ribshore | gannet, gannet_harbor, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore | Fire brick, blackened with old soot: the try-works where the blubber was boiled. |
| ? | tupelo_leaves | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Water tupelo leaves: broad, a dull mid green. |

| 207 | reef_rock | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | Rocky reef under the kelp: dark stone crusted with pink coralline and urchins. |
| 208 | prickly_pear_pad | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Prickly pear pads: grey-green with rows of pale spine clusters. |
| 209 | prickly_pear_fruit | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Prickly pear fruit and flowers: magenta tunas, with a few yellow blooms. |
| 210 | cholla | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Cholla: a pale straw glow of dense spines over green joints. |
| 211 | agave | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Agave leaves: thick, blue-grey, spine-tipped. |
| 212 | beach_pea_leaves | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array | Beach pea: low trailing green leaves with magenta-purple pea flowers. |
| 226 | karst_dark | jadewater | gannet, jadewater, kitehold | A dark grey limestone, almost slate: some towers are cut from it. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 227 | karst_light | jadewater | jadewater | A very pale limestone, nearly white with a cream cast: the palest towers. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 228 | karst_blue | jadewater | jadewater, saltmirror | A blue-grey limestone, cold in colour: a few towers stand out in it. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 229 | vine_mantle | jadewater | jadewater | A tower wrapped in vines and moss so thickly no rock shows: solid, so it can be climbed. v2: faceted with the rock. |
| 230 | karst_limestone | jadewater | jadewater | Pale blue-grey layered limestone: the towers and the Old Wall. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 231 | karst_streaked | jadewater | jadewater | A darker band of the limestone, streaked grey-black where water runs down the faces. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 232 | karst_pale | jadewater | jadewater | The palest bed of the limestone, almost white, near the tops of the towers. v2: drawn in flat facets (surface nets lit like sloped ground) instead of smoothed into slopes, so tower walls and the Old Wall's cliffs read as smooth rock, not one-voxel steps. |
| 234 | bamboo_culm | jadewater | jadewater | Golden bamboo culms with green nodes. Passable: you push between the stalks. |
| 235 | giant_culm | jadewater | jadewater | Giant bamboo: thick green-gold culms banded at the nodes. |
| 236 | bamboo_leaves | jadewater | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Narrow yellow-green bamboo leaves in feathery sprays. |
| 237 | banyan_bark | jadewater | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Smooth grey banyan bark, fluted where the aerial roots have fused into the trunk. |
| 240 | lantern_film | jadewater | bonebight, jadewater, kitehold | The living film on cave ceilings and cenote overhangs: damp karst rock pricked with points of cold blue light, threads of it hanging down. v2: was bright cyan all over; now plain damp rock whose points of light the shader adds (glow_specks). |
| 241 | lumen_moss | jadewater | jadewater, kitehold | Moss on the cenote rims, faintly lit by the film beneath it. |
| 242 | lily_pad | jadewater | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock | Giant lily pad: thick, waxy green with pale radial veins. Firm enough to stand on. |
| 243 | lily_flower | jadewater | jadewater, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Pale pink-white lily petals that glow a little at the base. |
| 244 | coral_lacquer | jadewater | bonebight, jadewater | Coral-red lacquer on the Order's bridges and gates; it has not faded. |
| 245 | temple_wood | jadewater | jadewater, kitehold | Dark cedar of the temple's posts and beams, gone grey at the edges. |
| 246 | temple_roof | jadewater | jadewater, saurischia | Dark grey-green roof tiles in long rows. |
| 247 | shoji_paper | jadewater | jadewater | Paper screens on a fine wooden lattice, yellowed. |
| 248 | temple_stone | jadewater | bonebight, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia | Grey granite of the lanterns, statues and steps, lichened. |
| 249 | bamboo_litter | jadewater | jadewater | Pale gold drift of fallen bamboo leaves on the forest floor. |
| 250 | tower_grass | jadewater | jadewater | Short wind-cropped grass and moss on the tower tops and the top of the Old Wall. |
| 251 | jungle_floor | jadewater | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Dark jungle floor: leaf litter, roots and moss under the banyans. |
| 252 | river_gravel | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kitehold, ribshore, saurischia, windbarrow | Rounded grey-brown pebbles on a river bed, seen through the water. |
| 253 | river_mud | glassfall | bonebight, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, lowmouth, saurischia, windbarrow | Damp brown silt and sand along a river's waterline. |

## Plants

| name | from | used by | notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| acacia | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | umbrella: Thorn acacia: a flat-topped umbrella of fine grey-green leaf on a short trunk, the crown wider than the tree is tall. |
| agave | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | canes: Agave: a rosette of thick blue-grey spine-tipped leaves splayed from the ground. |
| alder | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, peregrine, ribshore, windbarrow | branching: Wet ground, nitrogen fixer. |
| apple | gannet | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | branching: Orchard apple: a low, wide, spreading tree pruned open in the middle, crooked grey-brown limbs, red apples in autumn lying in a few heads over the top of the crown (seen best from above), its leaves going dull yellow. |
| araucaria | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | branching: Monkey-puzzle: a Jurassic conifer, a tall straight grey trunk bare for most of its height under an irregular, lopsided crown of rope-thick limbs that sweep out and curl up at the tips in loose tiers, clad in hard dark scales. Height and crown differ tree to tree. The Grimwold's canopy, standing over everything else. |
| ash_poppy | kitehold | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock | herb: Ash poppies: orange cups on the Plain's ash soil and the edges of the old lava. |
| aspen | glassfall | glassfall, redrock, vermilion | Pioneer of burn edges; half of them turn gold. |
| bald_cypress | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | branching: Bald cypress: a tall flat-crowned tree standing in the black water on a great flared, buttressed foot. Trees differ: some a straight spar under a narrow crown, some broad and lopsided, old ones leaning with a ragged top. |
| banyan | jadewater | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: Banyan: a huge spreading fig on a buttressed, fluted grey trunk; grows out of cliffs as cliff_banyan. |
| baobab | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: Baobab: a smooth grey column or barrel of a trunk, 3 to 6 m through and hardly narrowing, that stops abruptly in a flat-topped crown of a few thick limbs spreading out and turning up like roots. Sparse tufts at the tips; bare in the dry season (winter), when the limbs show against the sky. |
| barrel_sponge | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | sponge: A barrel sponge: one squat orange barrel, older than anything round it. |
| beach_pea | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array | shrub: Beach pea: a low mat of trailing leaves and purple pea flowers on the sand above the tide line. |
| beech | - | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | branching: A tall beech of the Hollins and the Edge hangers: a smooth, straight grey column rising into a high, dense dome of fresh green. |
| bigleaf_maple | city | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The Northwest's big maple: green, broad, mossy-limbed. Street and yard tree. Goes gold in autumn, now and then orange. v2: was the sugar maple's red. |
| birch | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | branching: Light-loving, quick to colonise. |
| black_mangrove | lowmouth | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock | branching: Black mangrove: a bushy tree on the mud banks just above the tide. |
| blackthorn | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, windbarrow | canes: Blackthorn: a dense thicket of near-black thorny stems, small leaves, sloes thick along the stems in autumn. Hedges and the field corners. |
| blossom_vine | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's vine: longer strands hung with rose-lilac blossom, from the trees only. |
| bluebell | - | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | herb: Bluebells: drifts of violet-blue flowers on the floor of the Hollins. |
| boulder_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_mound: Boulder coral: big rose-coloured domes, some as big as a car, a few smaller ones grown against them. |
| bracken | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, kitehold_rock, saurischia | herb: Bracken: waist-high fronds in sheets at the wood's edges and on the rising ground. |
| brain_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_mound: Brain coral: an olive dome scored with meandering grooves. |
| bramble | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | canes: Bramble: a sprawl of purple-red prickly canes arching over and down, dark leaves, blackberries along the canes in late summer and autumn. In hedges, glades and the field edges. |
| broken_snag | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow, lowmouth, redrock, saltmirror, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | snag: The lower trunk of a dead tree snapped off two to four metres up, bark still on, leaning a little. |
| bromeliad | kitehold | kitehold | crust: Tank bromeliads on the trunks and limbs of the cloud forest. A crust plant; its biome density is the chance a tree carries a colony. |
| broom | windbarrow | windbarrow | canes: Broom: a fountain of green whip-like switches, nearly leafless, crowded along their length with yellow pea flowers in late spring and early summer. On the chalk and the poor ground. |
| bull_kelp | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | Bull kelp: one bare whip of a stipe from the rock to the surface, a round float on top and long blades streaming from it along the surface. Grows only under 3 to 16 m of sea; height is the share of that depth (at high water) the stipe reaches; the rest of its length lies along the surface and gets longer as the tide goes out. Loose strands, not voxels, so swimmers pass through. |
| buttonbush | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | canes: Buttonbush: a leaning shrub of grey stems at the water's edge, glossy leaves, white pincushion flower balls at the tips in summer. |
| cabbage_palm | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | umbrella: Cabbage palm: a ringed grey trunk with a round head of stiff fan fronds (sprite fans round the head). |
| canopy_laurel | - | kitehold | branching: The main canopy under the emergents: tall, slim, dark-leaved broadleaf trees that close the sky at thirty metres. |
| cardon | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | cactus: Cardon: the tallest of the columnar cacti, grey-green and deep-ribbed, its arms leaving the trunk low and climbing beside it like organ pipes. On the gravel fans with the saguaros. |
| cattail | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | herb: Cattails: tall strap leaves in clumps in the shallows, most with a stem carrying a brown velvet head; drawn as sprites, so a kayak slides through them. |
| cholla | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | branching: Cholla: a small shrubby cactus of short jointed arms, pale with dense spines that glow against the light. |
| cliff_banyan | jadewater | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: A banyan that started in a crack in a cliff: smaller, grown out of the rock face on steep ground. |
| common_reed | thornpan | bonebight, gannet, kitehold, redrock, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | canes: Common reed (phragmites): tall straight canes in beds along the water, grey-green blades in tufts up their top half. |
| cordgrass | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock | herb: Smooth cordgrass (spartina): stiff upright blades in dense stands along the low salt marsh, standing in the tide to its knees at high water. Drawn with the sawgrass blades, which keep their own tawny green in every season. You and a kayak go through it. |
| cottage_rose | - | gannet, gannet_harbor, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold | canes: Roses in the cottage gardens and wild dog rose in the hedges: prickly canes arching over, pink flowers in clusters at their tips in summer. |
| cottonwood | redrock | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, vermilion | branching: Cottonwood: big spreading trees along the river where there is water. |
| cycad | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | herb: Cycad: a stiff rosette of dark glossy fronds springing from a squat scaly stump, waist to head high (drawn as sprites). |
| cypress_snag | lowmouth | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock | snag: A dead cypress, grey and barkless: the ghost forest where salt water reached the old swamp. |
| dawn_redwood | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | conifer: Dawn redwood: a deciduous redwood with a fluted, buttressed red trunk that flares at the foot and a narrow conical crown of soft feathery sprays that turn copper in autumn and fall, leaving the bare trunk and limbs in winter. Crowns vary in height, lean and limb spacing. Wet ground in the Fernwood. |
| dead_baobab | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | snag: A baobab that died standing: the same grey barrel with its limbs bare and broken short. On the pan rim they stand in groups, holding nothing. |
| dead_juniper | redrock | redrock, vermilion | snag: A silvered dead juniper, twisted, still standing. |
| desert_willow | redrock | redrock, vermilion | canes: Desert willow: a few thin leaning stems in the washes, narrow light-green leaves, lilac trumpet flowers at the tips in spring and summer. |
| drowned_pole | ribshore | ribshore | snag: A drowned spruce that has lost its limbs: a bare silver pole, broken off high, a few stubs left. |
| drowned_spruce | ribshore | ribshore | branching: A spruce the sea killed three hundred years ago and left standing: a thin silver pole up to twenty metres tall, a few dead limbs high up, its foot in the water. |
| drowned_stump | ribshore | ribshore | snag: The stump of a spruce the sea drowned three hundred years ago: silver, thick, root-flared, its top snapped off jagged. |
| dwarf_birch | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | canes: Dwarf birch: knee-high scrub of dark red-brown stems on the fell, small round leaves that go red and gold, bare twigs over the snow in winter. You walk through it. |
| elder | - | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia, windbarrow | branching: Elder in hedges and at the wood's edge: several arching stems from one root, flat tiers of leaves, and cream corymbs lying on top at midsummer. |
| elkhorn_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_antler: Elkhorn: a few thick limbs flattening into broad palms that face up into the surge, on the crest where the swell breaks. |
| ephedra | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | canes: Mormon tea (ephedra): a broom of jointed yellow-green stems knee to waist high, leafless, on the sand and the slickrock benches. You walk through it. |
| fallen_log | kitehold | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock, vermilion | snag: A tree that came down and lies where it fell, bark going grey, a limb or two snapped short. Under placement v2 a wood's own trees fall as logs of their own size and bark; listed on its own, it is this. |
| feather_tussock | windbarrow | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | herb: A big feathergrass tussock standing out of the sward, its awns silver-gold and bent downwind; drawn as sprites, so you walk through it. v2: three muted coats in drifts about 35 m across. |
| fern | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | herb: Sword fern on the forest floor: a ring of arching fronds drawn as sprites. |
| finger_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_finger: Finger coral: a low gold cushion bristling with short blunt fingers. |
| fir | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, saurischia | columnar: Tall, narrow, dark: the high slopes. |
| fireweed | glassfall | glassfall, kitehold, redrock, saurischia | herb: First colour back after a burn: clumps of magenta spikes drawn as sprites. |
| flax | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | herb: Flax grown for linen and kite cloth: knee-high stems with sky-blue flowers. |
| flowering_cherry | city | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia | branching: Ornamental cherry in a planting strip: low, wide, pink. |
| foxglove | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | herb: Foxglove: tall spikes of pink-purple bells in wood clearings and at the wood's edge; folklore calls them fairy gloves. |
| frailejon | kitehold | kitehold | umbrella: Frailejón (Espeletia): an old one, a silver rosette of furred leaves held up on a trunk shagged with the dead leaves of every year it has grown, two to four metres tall, standing over the young ones. |
| frailejon_rosette | kitehold | kitehold | herb: A young frailejón: a stiff upturned rosette of furred silver-green leaves on a short shaggy stem, knee to chest high, standing in its thousands across the páramo (drawn as sprites). |
| gambel_oak | redrock | redrock, vermilion | branching: Gambel oak brush under the pines and on the plateau edge. |
| giant_bamboo | jadewater | jadewater | bamboo: Giant bamboo: clumps of thick green-gold culms 14-22 m tall, nodding at the tips, leaves on the upper half. Solid. |
| giant_douglas | - | gannet, hemlow | conifer: An old Douglas fir: a straight furrowed column bare for more than half its height, then ragged tiers of dark limbs to a broken top. Height, lean, crown width, tier count and where the crown starts vary from tree to tree. Beside the redwoods, the other host tree for the treehouses. |
| giant_fir | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | An old fir well above the canopy; a landmark on the slopes. Old and wind-beaten: its whorls no longer sit level. |
| giant_horsetail | - | hemlow, saurischia | canes: Giant horsetail: clumps of jointed green stems taller than a man, whorled at every node, on wet ground. |
| giant_kapok | - | thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: The emergent of the canopy town: a straight grey column on great plank buttresses, clear for forty metres, opening into a flat umbrella of limbs above every other crown. The host tree for the walkways. |
| giant_redwood | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold | conifer: An old-growth redwood twice the height of the valley groves: a fluted red column bare for half its height, the crown a drooping spire above the broadleaf canopy. |
| ginkgo | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | branching: Ginkgo: an irregular, open, often lopsided crown on a few strong ascending limbs, fan leaves fresh green in summer and clear gold in autumn, dropped all at once. Old trees are broad and may stand on several trunks, young ones narrow and gawky. Older than the dinosaurs. |
| glass_fan | underwater | bonebight, glassfall | A fan of glass threads standing across the swell. |
| glass_staghorn | underwater | bonebight, glassfall | coral_branch: Glassfall's strange staghorn: clear cyan glass branches, faintly lit, nearest the strewn field. |
| glass_table | underwater | bonebight, glassfall | coral_table: A table of lilac glass, light coming through its plate. |
| glassbloom | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | herb: A shrub whose leaves are hard and glassy: a fan of faceted violet blades, drawn as sprites. Found only near skystone. |
| glasswort | saltmirror | gannet, kitehold, saltmirror, saltmirror_array | shrub: Glasswort: a low mat of jointed red succulent stems round the brine, the only thing that grows where the salt is wet. |
| gold_maple | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia | A maple whose leaves stay gold all year: the first sign of the change. |
| golden_bamboo | jadewater | jadewater | bamboo: Golden bamboo of the gorges: dense clumps of thin culms 6-11 m tall that you push through (the culms are passable). |
| gorse | - | bonebight, gannet, hemlow, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand | canes: Gorse: dense spiny dark green bushes, yellow with flowers most of the year, on the heath of the rising ground. |
| great_oak | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | branching: An old, tall oak: a wide domed crown of foliage over visible spreading limbs, hollow underneath. |
| grim_fern | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | Ferns on the Grimwold floor: the ordinary sword fern's shape in a darker, duller green. |
| hanging_vine | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, redrock, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | vine: A climbing vine that has reached the top and hangs back down: strands from the crowns of broadleaf trees, or a curtain over the lip of a cliff. `height` is the strand length, `crown_radius` the width of a cliff curtain. |
| hawthorn | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, peregrine, redrock, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow, zoo | branching: Hawthorn: a small, hard, twiggy tree of hedges, field corners and wood edge, often several stems from one root, smothered in white blossom in spring. |
| hazel | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia | canes: Hazel: a coppiced stool of straight grey-brown stems fanning up from one root, leaves in tufts, catkins on the bare stems in late winter. |
| heather | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, windbarrow | herb: Low purple shrub of poor, open ground: a mat of wiry stems with mauve bells, drawn as sprites. |
| holly | - | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | branching: Holly: a small dark evergreen tree of the Hollins understory, glossy spined leaves and red berries in winter. The tree the Hollins are named for. |
| huckleberry | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, ribshore | canes: Red huckleberry: a loose knee-to-waist-high bush of bright green angled stems arching over, small oval leaves, red berries in summer. You walk through it. |
| hydrangea | bonebight | bonebight | branching: Mophead hydrangea: a rounded bush one to two metres high, dark leaves under big blue flower heads, planted along Vila Azul's walls and hedges as the Azoreans did at home. Bare in winter. |
| juniper | redrock | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, vermilion, windbarrow | gnarled: Utah juniper: a short leaning trunk that splits low into twisted limbs, each ending in a flat clump of scale leaves; often one limb is dead. Grows out of cracks in bare rock. |
| karst_pine | jadewater | jadewater | umbrella: The small wind-twisted pine of the tower tops: flat-crowned, 3-6 m. |
| karst_vine | jadewater | jadewater | vine: The long vine curtains of the karst: strands 5-16 m down every tower lip and ledge. |
| kelp | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | kelp: Giant kelp: stipes rising from a holdfast on the reef to the surface, blades all the way up and a floating canopy. Grows only under 3 to 16 m of sea; height is the share of that depth (at high water) the stipe reaches; the rest of its length lies along the surface and gets longer as the tide goes out. Loose strands, not voxels, so swimmers pass through. |
| liana | - | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | A woody climber that has reached the canopy and hangs back down to the floor: long strands from the big crowns. |
| lightning_pine | glassfall | unused | snag: A pine killed by lightning and burnt black, still standing among the living ones. About one pine in fifty. |
| live_oak | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: Southern live oak: a vast low dome on a short black trunk, limbs reaching out sideways, hung with moss. |
| lophelia | underwater | kaldvik | coral_branch: Lophelia: white cold-water coral in bushy thickets on the deep fjord walls, red fans and cod among it. |
| lowbush_blueberry | gannet | gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, ribshore | canes: Lowbush blueberry: an ankle-to-knee mat of thin red-brown twigs and small blue-green leaves on poor sandy ground, dusty blue berries in late summer, and in autumn the whole barren goes crimson. You walk through it. |
| lupine | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, kitehold, kitehold_rock, ribshore, ribstrand, zoo | herb: Purple lupine in drifts on the dunes, drawn as sprites. |
| maple | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | branching: A maple: a straight trunk under a dense, rounded crown. In autumn each tree or pair of trees turns its own colour: mostly scarlet, crimson or orange, some ochre gold, a few still olive. v3: autumn coats instead of one red for the whole wood. |
| momiji | bonebight | bonebight | A Japanese maple: a small tree four to seven metres high, its fine limbs sweeping out in flat tiers, green in summer and deep crimson in autumn. Planted round the temple on Kagami Pond. |
| mopane | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | branching: Mopane: a scrubby thicket of butterfly leaf on thin stems, head high, on the thinnest sand. Turns coppery in the dry. |
| mother_oak | windbarrow | hemlow, windbarrow | branching: The great oak below Durn: a vast domed crown on a short, hugely buttressed black trunk, wider than it is tall and old enough that the village has always called it that. One tree only, in its own region. |
| oak | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | branching: A broad, spreading oak on a stout buttressed trunk: heavy crooked limbs under a wide, domed crown wider than the tree is tall. |
| ocotillo | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | canes: Ocotillo: a fan of whip-like thorny canes from one root crown, set with small leaves along their length (leaf cards on the cane voxels, coloured by ocotillo_leaves) and tipped with scarlet flames of flowers in spring only (flower_seasons), drawn as sprites on ocotillo_flower marker voxels. |
| osier | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, redrock, vermilion, windbarrow | canes: Osier willow: a stool of long straight orange-red withies by the water, narrow leaves in tufts, cut for baskets. Brightest bare in winter. |
| paintbrush | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | herb: Indian paintbrush: scarlet bracts in drifts along the Plain's buried stream beds. |
| pale_birch | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | A birch bleached by the bloom. |
| pale_kelp | bonebight | bonebight | Giant kelp gone pale near the black glass in Frondwell's lagoon: the same stipes and canopy, bleached to bone white and a faint violet. |
| papyrus | thornpan | redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | canes: Papyrus: dense stands of bright green stems twice a man's height, each topped with a mop of fine rays arching out and over, along the channels. |
| pincushion | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | canes: Pincushion protea: a low rounded bush with orange flower heads like pincushions all over its top in spring. |
| pine | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array | conifer: Upland conifer. Branches thrown up at the ends, and a less regular cone than a fir. |
| pinyon | redrock | redrock, vermilion | branching: Pinyon pine: small, round and bushy, on the benches between the cliffs. |
| plane_tree | saurischia | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | A boulevard plane: a tall clean trunk of flaking pale bark, limbs rising then spreading under a broad high dome of big leaves. |
| plate_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_plate: Plate coral: violet plates overlapping in a whorl, like a head of lettuce. |
| podocarp | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | shrub: Podocarp scrub: a dense, dark, many-stemmed conifer bush two to four metres high, the old southern relative of the yew. Makes the Grimwold's thickets. |
| ponderosa | redrock | kitehold, redrock, saurischia, vermilion | conifer: Mature ponderosa pine: a straight orange trunk bare for a third to half its height, tapering into a stack of rounded needle tiers centred on it, small at the foot, widest about two fifths up and small again at the blunt top, with sky between each tier. Tier count, bare length and width vary tree to tree. |
| prickly_pear | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | pads: Prickly pear: a low sprawl of flat jointed pads, magenta fruit on the top ones. Bees visit its flowers. |
| protea | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | canes: King protea: a bush of stiff grey-green stems, each one ending in a cup-shaped pink flower head the size of a plate, from winter into spring. The burn round the wreck is thick with them. |
| rabbitbrush | redrock | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, kitehold_rock, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | canes: Rubber rabbitbrush: a rounded bush of many upright felted grey-green stems, each topped with mustard-yellow flower heads in late summer and autumn, in washes and disturbed ground. You walk through it. No leaf tufts (cane_leaves 0): the rabbitbrush leaf material is a herb marker that would grow a whole bush sprite on every tuft voxel. The flower heads are rabbitbrush_flowers marker voxels drawn as small yellow sprays (mesh/sprites.rs). |
| red_mangrove | lowmouth | jadewater, lowmouth | mangrove: Red mangrove: a dense dark rounded crown, low and wide, on a tangle of arching prop roots with drop roots from the limbs, standing in the shallows of the river mouth. |
| red_osier | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore | canes: Red-osier dogwood: thickets of bright red stems in the wet hollows and along the creeks, leaves in tufts, red against the grey all winter. |
| redwood | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold | conifer: Coast redwood: the tallest trees in the library, a fluted red trunk bare for half its height and a narrow dark crown. Groves in the wet lowland valleys. Each tree differs in height, lean, crown depth and limb spacing; the limbs are ragged, with gaps, and the tops are uneven. |
| redwood_sorrel | - | bonebight, gannet, hemlow, kitehold | herb: Redwood sorrel: bright clover-like leaves carpeting the redwood floor. |
| restio | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | herb: Restio: tussocks of jointed reed, tawny and green, the grass of the fynbos. Drawn as sprites. |
| rock_lichen | base | gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | crust: Lichen on bare rock: pale crusts on cliff and boulder faces, on every aspect, so it is not limited to the shaded side. |
| rock_moss | base | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | crust: Moss on the shaded faces of rock: patches down a bank or a crag that never gets the midday sun. A crust plant; its density is the chance per 4 m cell of steep ground. |
| rock_rose | windbarrow | megalith, peregrine, windbarrow | herb: Common rock-rose: sprawling clumps of yellow flowers on sunny chalk turf, with the thyme. |
| rowan | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock | branching: Rowan: a slender small tree with feathery leaves and heavy bunches of red berries in autumn, planted at farm gates against witches. |
| sagebrush | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | shrub: Big sagebrush, the grey-green cover of the open desert. |
| saguaro | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | cactus: Saguaro: a ribbed green column with a rounded top; old ones carry up to five arms that leave the trunk level and turn up. |
| salal | city | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, ribshore | shrub: Salal: a knee-high evergreen shrub under trees and along yards and strips. |
| sapling_broadleaf | kitehold | unused | branching: A young broadleaf near the tree that seeded it (placement v2 stand structure): a thin stem and a small open crown, head high to twice that. It wears its parent's bark, leaves and autumn colour, so one shape stands for every broadleaf's young. |
| sapling_conifer | kitehold | unused | sapling: A young conifer near the tree that seeded it (placement v2 stand structure): a narrow cone from knee to twice head height. It wears its parent's bark and needles, so one shape stands for every conifer's young. |
| sapling_fir | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, ribshore | sapling: A young fir in the understorey: a narrow dark cone from knee to twice head height. |
| sapling_maple | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, kitehold | A young broadleaf, head high to twice that, waiting in the shade for a gap in the canopy. v2: turns the same reds, oranges and golds as the grown maples instead of the generic autumn leaf. |
| sapling_pine | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock | sapling: Young pine coming back after fire. Bushy and upswept, like the pines it will grow into. |
| saw_palmetto | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | herb: Saw palmetto: low clumps of grey-green fans under the oaks and on the hammocks, drawn as sprites. |
| sawgrass | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | herb: Sawgrass: low tussocks of toothed tawny blades out of the shallow water, drawn as sprites (a kayak slides through them). |
| sea_buckthorn | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | canes: Sea buckthorn in thickets on the dunes: stiff thorny canes rising upright and arching over, sparse silver leaves, the stems crowded with orange berries from midsummer through autumn, bare grey in winter. |
| sea_fan | underwater | bonebight, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik | sea_fan: A sea fan: a purple lattice spread flat across the swell; the fans on one reef all face the same way. |
| sea_fan_red | underwater | glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik | sea_fan: A red sea fan, on the deeper wall. |
| sea_whip | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | canes: Sea whips: a clump of thin unbranched rods a metre or two long, orange-yellow, leaning and curving with the surge. Under 3 to 25 m of sea. |
| sea_whip_violet | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | Violet sea whips, the same rods in a cooler colour, deeper on the wall. |
| seed_fern | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | canes: Seed fern: a Mesozoic shrub of arching woody stems clad in big fern fronds, head high, in tangles under the monkey-puzzles. |
| shelf_fungus | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | crust: Bracket fungus on old trunks: half-shelves standing out from the bark in twos and threes. A crust plant, so its biome density is the chance a tree carries a colony, and `height` the band of trunk it keeps to. |
| shore_pine | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand | gnarled: Shore pine: a low pine the wind twists, leaning off the sea, its trunk splitting low into crooked limbs that end in flat pads of needles, the whole crown wider than it is tall. |
| silver_tree | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | branching: Silver tree: a small upright tree of the ravines whose silky leaves turn the whole crown silver in the wind. |
| sitka_spruce | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, hemlow, kitehold, ribshore | conifer: Sitka spruce at the edge of the forest: tall, dark and blue-green, salt-tolerant, the coast's own tree. Heights, leans and tier counts differ tree to tree, and the whorls sit ragged, some limbs drooping and some held out, with gaps between tiers. |
| small_fig | jadewater | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | broad: A small fig tree, 3-5 m, that grows anywhere it gets a root in: tower tops, ledges, the backs of beaches. |
| snag | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | snag: Standing dead tree, often fire-killed. |
| spanish_moss | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | vine: Spanish moss: grey beards hanging from the crowns of the live oaks and cypress. |
| spiral_pine | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | spiral: Grows in a slow helix and comes out pale where the strangeness is strong. Nobody knows why. |
| staghorn_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_branch: Staghorn: limbs forking up and out from one foot, ochre with pale blunt tips; in thickets on the fore reef. |
| staghorn_violet | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_branch: A violet staghorn, the same build in a cooler colour. |
| strangler_fig | - | jadewater | branching: A fig that started high in another tree and sent its roots down round it: a fluted, many-stemmed grey trunk and one broad cloud of a crown under the emergents. |
| street_maple | city | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | A planted street maple: smaller and tidier than the bigleaf, a few leaves turning, its trunk tapering into smooth limbs. v2: tapered trunk and tube limbs. v3: the few trees turning early are now a morph, and in autumn each tree goes mostly scarlet, crimson or orange, some ochre gold, a few still olive. |
| street_oak | city | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | A red oak planted along a street: tall and round, its trunk narrowing clearly into smooth limbs under a dark green crown. v2: tapered trunk and tube limbs. |
| stump | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, redrock, vermilion | snag: A knee-high stump where a tree snapped off long ago, grey bark still on. |
| sugar_cane | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | canes: Sugar cane: dense clumps of straight jointed stalks taller than a man, banded yellow-green and purple, ribbon leaves at their tops. You push through it. |
| sunflower | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | herb: Sunflowers grown for oil on Kitehold's farms: head-high stalks with yellow faces. |
| switchcane | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | canes: Switchcane: the delta's own bamboo, canebrakes of thin olive culms two to five metres tall under the bottomland trees. You push through it. |
| table_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_table: Table coral: a stalk and a wide flat plate, fish hanging in its shade. |
| table_coral_teal | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | coral_table: A table coral the jade colour of the water. |
| tamarisk | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | canes: Tamarisk thickets on the lake shore and the lower river: many thin red-brown stems arching out, feathery grey-green foliage, pink plumes at the tips in spring and summer. |
| thimbleberry | glassfall | glassfall, redrock, vermilion | canes: Thimbleberry: a waist-high patch of unarmed canes with big maple-like leaves, red thimble fruit at the tips in summer, in clearings and along forest edges. You walk through it. |
| titan_araucaria | saurischia | saurischia | The oldest monkey-puzzles in the Titan Stand: 38-46 m, far thicker in the trunk than the Grimwold's, with a heavy root flare, bare for most of its height under a broad, lopsided crown of long upturned limbs in loose tiers. |
| tree_fern | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | umbrella: Tree fern: a slender fibrous brown trunk from two to six metres high, some short, some tall, under a spreading crown of long arching fronds. |
| tree_moss | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | crust: Moss up the shaded side of a trunk: patches of green felt on the north face. A crust plant; its biome density is the chance a tree carries it. |
| trillium | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold | herb: Western trillium: three broad leaves and one white flower, scattered on the redwood floor. |
| tube_sponge | underwater | bonebight, glassfall, jadewater | sponge: Tube sponges: fat purple tubes standing in a cluster. |
| umbrella_pine | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array | umbrella: Wind-shaped coastal pine with a flat crown. |
| wall_plate_coral | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | crust: Plate coral shelving out of a reef wall in tiers. A crust plant: its biome density is the chance a 4 m stretch of wall carries a colony, and it keeps to the depths it names. |
| water_lily | lowmouth | bonebight, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, thornpan, thornpan_delta | herb: Water lily: a root on the bed in still shallow water, drawn as a raft of pads lying flat on the surface with now and then a bloom. Sprites only, so a boat slides over it. |
| water_tupelo | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | branching: Water tupelo: a swollen-footed swamp tree with a narrow crown of light green, among the cypress. |
| wild_thyme | windbarrow | megalith, peregrine, windbarrow | herb: Wild thyme: low mats of pink-purple flowers through the short turf of the chalk downs, where heather, which hates lime, does not grow. |
| willow | glassfall | bonebight, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, vermilion, windbarrow | weeping: River banks. |
| wisteria | faerie | hemlow, jadewater | vine: Wisteria: a woody vine hung with long racemes, over trees and down cliffs. It comes in three colours, each cluster one of them: violet, lilac and a rare white. v3: the colours are morphs rather than the one lilac material (not drawn yet). v2: was a weeping tree; worlds that had it plant flowering_cherry instead. |
| yew | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, saurischia | branching: Yew: a squat, very dark evergreen with a fluted red-brown trunk and a dense rounded crown of flat needles. An ancient family, and the one tree the Grimwold shares with the Hollins; it grows thickest along the top of Raven Scar. |
| young_araucaria | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | conifer: A young monkey-puzzle under 15 m: a narrow cone clothed to near the ground in whorls of dark, up-curving scaly branches. The Fernwood's future canopy. |
| young_ponderosa | redrock | kitehold, redrock, saurischia, vermilion | conifer: Ponderosa pine under about eighty years, still cone-shaped, crown starting a third of the way up. |
| young_redwood | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold | conifer: A redwood of a century or so, already taller than the firs round it. |
| yucca | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | shrub: Narrowleaf yucca: a stiff clump of blades on sandy slopes. |

## Structures

| name | from | used by | notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| abbey_ruin | - | hemlow | The roofless ruin of a honey-stone abbey church 44 m by 16 m, its east gable standing 14 m round an empty pointed window, the nave walls broken to 1.5-9 m over the stumps of two rows of round piers (one fallen across the nave), and on the south (local -X) the grassed footings of a 24 m cloister with four arches still standing. Front (the west door) faces local -Z; carves nothing. |
| aframe_cabin | saltmirror | kaldvik_fjord, saltmirror | A staff A-frame (1968), 7.5 m wide and 10 m long: a steep shingle roof down to a plank deck, the front gable (local -Z) glazed with a 1.5 m by 2.5 m doorway and a deck rail under the overhang, a sleeping loft at the back and a brick-based stove whose pipe goes through the ridge. |
| almshouse_row | - | hemlow | A single-storey row of four almshouses, each 3.5 m wide and 5 m deep, with a 1 m by 2.25 m painted door and a window apiece under one shared stone-slate pentice on oak posts, and a chapel bay at the right end with a 5 m wide stepped stone gable and bellcote. 21 m by 5.5 m walls, 5.5 m to the ridge. Faces local -Z; a stack for each house. Carves nothing. |
| anemone_bed | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | Giant green anemones packed in a pool, jade columns with pale tentacles. |
| arch_bridge | redrock | redrock | The 1929 steel deck-arch bridge over Sorrel Canyon: 88 m long and 8.5 m wide along local X, twin lattice ribs springing from concrete skewbacks 20 m below the deck, braced columns carrying a concrete deck with a 6 m asphalt road, 1 m footways and 1.25 m open railings. Faces along X (road runs west-east); it carves nothing and base rim sets the deck on the canyon rims. |
| arch_double | redrock | vermilion | A sandstone fin 30 m long and up to 16.5 m high, pierced twice (10.5 m and 10 m wide) with a 3 m pillar between and a small window above it. Faces -Z; long axis local X, carves nothing. |
| arch_high_window | redrock | vermilion | A tall sandstone fin 20 m long, 6 m deep and 26 m high with a window 8 m wide and 7.5 m high, its sill 12 m up, through it. Faces local -Z (the broad face); the fin is banded, pitted and leans a little in x. |
| arch_pothole | redrock | vermilion | A squat sandstone dome 18 m by 16 m and 10 m high. A pothole worn down from its crown meets a tunnel through the base along Z, open at both ends, so you can look up the shaft from inside. |
| arch_ribbon | redrock | vermilion | A long thin sandstone ribbon 35 m across and 12.5 m high, 4 m deep at the feet, with a span about 2 m thick and 1.75 m wide at the crown over an opening 26.5 m wide. Faces local -Z; long axis local X. |
| arch_span | redrock | vermilion | A broad, heavy sandstone arch 28 m across and 14.4 m high, 5.5 m deep, its span about 5.5 m thick over an opening 16.5 m wide and 8 m high. Faces local -Z; long axis local X, standing on slickrock benches with talus and fallen slabs at the feet. |
| arch_young | redrock | vermilion | A sandstone fin 16 m long and up to 15 m high with a deep alcove (7 m wide, 7.5 m tall) eaten into its -Z face and a small ragged hole just broken through the thin back wall. Long axis local X. |

| arena_transmission | arena_transmission | transmission | The whole of the hand-drawn transmission arena as one structure, built by tools/arena. Written by the tool, not by hand: edit the map, not this file. |
| array_dish | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | One of the eleven Saltmirror Array dishes: a 100 m bowl on a leaning four-legged pedestal that rises 20 m to the turntable, with a quadripod holding the feed cabin 80 m up. Stairs on the back (local +Z) climb in flights to a bridge, a ladder goes up the drum to the turntable and on up the west arm; an equipment hut on the pad has a 1 m by 2.25 m doorway on its east side. Faces zenith; carves nothing. |
| array_dish_tilted | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | A Saltmirror Array dish parked part-way down, its 100 m bowl tipped 35 degrees off the zenith toward the front (local -Z), on the same pedestal, stairs (local +Z), turntable ladder and equipment hut as the upright dish. Carves nothing. |
| assay_office | redrock | redrock | Harlow's brick assay office, 5 m wide and 6.5 m deep with 0.5 m brick walls and a low tin gable roof, a 1 m by 2.5 m door (open leaf inside), barred windows, a counter with an iron grille and a back strongroom with a safe. Front is -Z; carves nothing. |
| bait_shop | lowmouth | lowmouth | Theriot's bait and tackle at the river landing: a board shack 4 m wide on 0.75 m piers behind a 2 m porch, 4.5 m by 7.8 m overall, under one tin gable with a painted sign on the front. Faces local -Z; the red door is 1 m by 2.25 m, no carving. |
| bakehouse | - | hemlow | A village bakehouse, 6 m by 4.5 m walls of honey stone under a stone-slate roof, 5 m to the ridge, with a 1 m by 2.5 m door and mullioned front window. A domed stone oven on a 1 m plinth bulges from the right end, 9 m long overall, its mouth open into the room, with a flue on top. Faces local -Z. Carves nothing. |
| banding_hut | gannet | unused | Weathered timber puffin banding hut, 3 m by 4 m with a tar-paper roof, an open door (1.25 m by 2.5 m) on the front (local -Z), a window on the east side, a stove and stovepipe, a desk and shelves of record boxes inside and a bench outside. It carves nothing. |
| baobab_cistern | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The cistern tree: a hollow baobab sealed with clay at the bottom and kept full of water against the end of the dry, with pegs hammered up the outside and a low door to dip from. Face the door downhill toward the path. |
| baobab_tomb | thornpan | thornpan | The tomb tree: a hollow baobab closed with a dressed slab, two generations of one family inside. Face the slab toward open ground; nothing opens it. |
| barge_wreck | lowmouth | lowmouth | A rusted cane barge, 5 m by 18 m, sunk on the river bank and broken across the middle, bow (local -Z) tapering to a point and a 3 m deckhouse at the stern (+Z) whose door faces forward onto the aft deck. | barrow_post | windbarrow | windbarrow | A split-oak post 3.5 m tall standing on a kurgan's crown, ringed with red ochre, chalk blocks packed round its foot. Broad faces along local X. |
| basalt_boulder | kaldvik | bonebight, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock, ribshore, saurischia | A boulder of black basalt the ice dropped where it melted, an erratic, rimed on its windward side. |
| basalt_columns | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, redrock, saurischia | A cluster of columnar basalt, the tops at different heights. |
| bb_boat_shed | bonebight | bonebight | A boat shed, 15 m by 8 m under a 45 degree silver shingle roof 8.5 m to the ridge, on a bone-block sill. Faces local -Z with a 4.5 m by 3.75 m double door standing open, its leaves swung back against the front wall. Inside, a 10 m boat is half built on stocks along the long axis: a driftwood keel and curved frames with planking on the lower half, beside a workbench, a steam box and a plank stack. Carves its interior. |
| bb_bone_shed | bonebight | bonebight | The bone-cutters' workshop, 12 m by 8 m: an open-fronted shed on driftwood posts on bone-block pads under a low-pitched silver shingle roof patched with red, with a saw bench, stacks of cut bone blocks, one rough titan-bone lump and a 4 m by 4 m enclosed office at the east end. Open to local -Z and the west end; the office door faces -Z. Carves its interior. |
| bb_drift_giant | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore | A storm-thrown cedar trunk about 38 m long, bleached silver, 3.2 m thick at the butt and tapering to a broken top, with limb stubs and cracks along the grain. It lies along local X and is built from logs and discs, so it can lie at any bearing. |
| bb_drift_giant_b | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore | A giant spruce trunk about 28 m long and 2.6 m thick, with its root plate standing on edge at the butt, about 6 m across, tangled root ends and sand caught in it. Built from logs and discs only; the plate is at local -X. |
| bb_drift_wall | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore | A heap of 19 storm-thrown trunks lying across each other, 30 m long and 5.5 m high, with gaps under the upper trunks that you can walk through and steps up the logs to the top. Built from logs and discs only. |
| bb_ferry_skiff | bonebight | bonebight | The chain ferry, 7.5 m long and 3 m across with its floats, a plank deck at y=6 over a hull with cobalt tile inlays, six green floats along each side, and a cedar-shingled roof on four posts. A marrow-crystal slab stands bolted to the deck amidships; chain fairleads sit at both ends and the long axis is local X. |
| bb_glass_ear | bonebight | bonebight | A cluster of glass-ear shelf fungi hung from the underside of a rib bone: twelve shelves 1 to 1.5 m across in four tiers 0.5 m apart, amber and teal, on a stalk 2.2 m long under a bone pad. It faces local -Z. |
| bb_marrow_works | bonebight | bonebight | A marrow mine head on a titan's hip bone: an 8 m timber headframe over a 3 m marrow-lined shaft on a 2 m bone plateau, a 3 m shingled winding shed beside it with a hoist rope, an ore cart on 5 m of track and a sorting bench with marrow crystals. Faces local -Z: bone steps climb to the plateau and the shed door is on its front. |
| bb_pen | bonebight | bonebight | A farm pen in the water, 13 m by 9 m: silver driftwood posts down to the bed, a plank catwalk with a rail round the edge 1.5 m above the sea, and a cross walk splitting it. The west half holds three rows of oyster racks with mesh bags; the east half is an open pen of six 4 m mooring posts with iron rings. Base sea, front local -Z. |
| bb_shingle_cottage | bonebight | bonebight | A one-and-a-half-storey shingled cottage, 5.5 m by 5.5 m with a 2 m deep lean-to porch on silver driftwood posts across the front, so 5.5 m by 7.5 m overall, under a gable roof with a dormer window on the front slope. Faces local -Z; a 1.25 m by 2.5 m open door leads from the porch into one room with a stair along the west wall up to the loft. Carves its interior. |
| bb_shingle_house | bonebight | bonebight | A two-storey cedar-shingle house, 7.5 m by 5 m with 3 m storeys under a steep silver-shingle gable roof patched with red, on a 0.75 m bone-block footing. Front faces local -Z with a 1.25 m by 2.5 m open door up a bone-block step; inside are a plank floor, a stair along the back wall to the upper floor, a partition with a doorway on each floor, small-paned windows and an iron stove with a stovepipe through the roof. Carves its interior. |
| bb_smokehouse | bonebight | bonebight | A tall plank smokehouse 7.5 m by 5 m in silver cedar shingle and driftwood, 3.5 m to the eaves and 7 m to the top of its ridge vent. Faces local -Z: a 1.5 m by 2.5 m open door leads into one room with a stone fire pit and six racks of hanging split salmon. |
| bb_vertebra | bonebight | bonebight | A titan vertebra weathered out of the dune, 9.5 m across its two processes and 7 m from its buried base to the top of the spine. The centrum lies on its side with its lower 1.3 m in the sand, and its broken front end shows marrow honeycomb. |
| bb_vertebra_b | bonebight | bonebight | A smaller titan vertebra tipped on its side, 6.8 m across and 5.5 m high, spine pointing right, one process standing and the other snapped off and lying on the ground beside it. The front end is broken and shows marrow. |
| bear_den | windbarrow | windbarrow | A cave bear's bed: a trodden floor of chalk rubble and bone 5 m across with long bones and a skull lying in it. | bee_bank | thornpan | thornpan | A carmine bee-eater colony: fifteen metres of cut earth bank honeycombed with nest holes, a sand apron at its foot. Face it at the water on the outside of a bend; place bee_eaters with it. |
| bell_house | jadewater | jadewater | An open bell house, 4.5 m square on a stepped stone plinth: posts and a rail round three sides, a tiled gable roof 5 m across, 7.8 m to the ridge and a 2 m green bronze bell hung from a beam. Open front on local -Z; carves nothing. |
| beset_ship | kaldvik | kaldvik | An old polar expedition barquentine, 53 m overall with the bowsprit and 7.75 m in the beam, caught in the cove ice and abandoned: a curved hull standing 5.5 m out of the floe with a raised poop, pressure ridges along both sides, masts leaning to starboard and sails frozen on the yards. Door in the poop's forward wall and in the deckhouse's aft wall (1 m by 2 m), stairs to the poop and down to the lower deck, which has 2.25 m headroom. Long axis local X, bow at +X; `base: sea`. |
| boarding_house | redrock | redrock | A Harlow boarding house, 8 m wide and 9 m deep plus a 2 m porch: two storeys of cream clapboard under a tin gable roof, a central hall with a stair, a parlour and a dining room below, six bedrooms above behind a row of five front windows, and a brick chimney. Front is -Z; carves nothing. |
| bone_arch | saurischia | saurischia | The neck of a diplodocus, about 18 m of vertebrae arching out of the ground and back into it, 4 m at the top, the big vertebrae at the local -X end and the small ones at +X, with short neck ribs; the skull lies by the +X end. |
| bone_fence | windbarrow | windbarrow | A 10 m run of stock fence built of mammoth ribs set upright in the chalk with a rail lashed along them, for ground where no hazel grows. Long axis local X. |
| bone_house | windbarrow | windbarrow | A house of mammoth where there is no timber: a footing ring of eight skulls with their sockets showing, ribs arched over them, hides lashed over the upper ribs only, five pairs of tusks arching clear over the roof and two more crossing over the doorway. 7 m across; door on local -Z. |
| bone_outcrop | windbarrow | windbarrow | A mammoth weathering out of the chalk: the dome of a skull, one tusk curving out of the ground and three ribs standing clear of it. Settles on the ground; faces local -Z. |
| bone_shard | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia | A length of broken rib or limb bone half sunk in the sand, its broken ends showing the honeycomb. |

| boulder | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | A rounded boulder, lichened. |
| brick_cistern | lowmouth | lowmouth | A round brick cistern 5.5 m across with a footing and a coping, that caught rainwater for the sugar house, open at the top, two rusted iron hoops, an iron ladder and a spigot on local -Z. Hollow inside, 2 voxels of wall. |
| brick_quarters | lowmouth | lowmouth | A roofless row of three brick rooms, 16 m by 5 m outside with 3 m walls, facing local -Z; each room has a 1.25 m by 2.5 m door and a window on the front, a window behind, a doorway through each partition, and the two inner rooms a fireplace under a chimney stack rising to 6 m. | brick_wall_ruin | lowmouth | lowmouth | The last wall of a brick building, standing alone with an arched doorway through it and buttresses at the ends; faces local -Z. |
| bridge_bay | saurischia | saurischia | One 16 m bay of a long stone bridge in the Charles Bridge manner: a pier with rounded cutwaters 11 m across, a segmental arch about 13.5 m wide, an 8 m paved deck between parapets, and over the pier a robed saint of dark stone with a gilt halo on a limestone plinth each side (one holds a cross, one a staff). | bridge_tower | saurischia | saurischia | The gothic gate tower at the shore end of a bridge: a dark sandstone block 12 x 13 m across its corner turrets and 34 m high, with a pointed gate through it along Z under a carved tympanum and a coat of arms, a band of blind arcading, three traceried windows a side, a pierced-parapet gallery under a steep slate pyramid roof, and four crocketed turrets. | bridge_troll | city | no shipped world | Landmark: a concrete troll under the north end of the bridge, clutching a car. |
| burrow_mound | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A prairie dog's burrow mound: a cone of bare packed soil 2.5 m across and 0.8 m high round a hole going straight down, on a pale apron of kicked-out soil 5 m across where nothing grows. | byre | windbarrow | hemlow, windbarrow | A Windbarrow cattle byre 5.5 m by 11 m under a straw gable 5.5 m to the ridge, daub walls on a fieldstone course with slit vents, six hurdle stalls down each side of a 1.5 m aisle and a manger rail. The wide end door, 2 m by 2.25 m, is on local -Z. |
| cairn | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold | Stacked stones marking a path or a summit. |
| camp_hearth | windbarrow | windbarrow | An open hearth in a village yard: a chalk-block kerb 3.5 m across with ash and charcoal in it and a tripod of poles over it. |
| campanile | saurischia | saurischia | A square brick bell tower 6 m on a side and 33 m to the finial, with limestone string courses, an open belfry of round arches on all four sides holding a bell, and a terracotta pyramid cap. | canal_bridge | saurischia | unused | A stone hump-backed bridge over a canal, 22 m long along local X and 6 m wide, one segmental arch of 14 m with a 1.25 m parapet each side; the deck rises in low steps from each bank towards the crown. | canal_house | saurischia | unused | A narrow Venetian house 7.5 m wide by 10 m deep, three storeys under a low terracotta roof, tan stucco with pale stone trim. | canal_lock | saurischia | saurischia | A canal lock gate for Cascadelle: two timber leaves meeting in a V across a 10.5 m canal between stone heads, a plank walk with an iron rail along their tops and a balance beam on each side; 14 m across (local X), 3 m of it below the quay. | cane_cottage_b | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | A side-gabled house 18 voxels (4.5 m) wide and 20 deep (5 m) on short timber posts, ridge running east-west, with a 1.75 m deep porch under a lean-to roof, a centred red door 1 m by 2.25 m between two windows, and a central brick stack. | cape_cod | gannet | gannet_harbor | A 1.5-storey Cape Cod house 10 m wide by 8 m deep, with grey weathered walls on a granite plinth, white trim, a 45-degree side-gabled roof and a big central brick chimney 10 m tall. | careened_hull | thornpan | thornpan | A two-masted sea-going ship, 22 metres from stem to taffrail and 6 in the beam, lying on the salt heeled fifty degrees onto her starboard side: bow to local -Z, deck turned to +X with the lower masts still standing out over the pan, barnacle crust over the bilge and her anchor on its chain beside the bow. | dory | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor | A Banks dory afloat, 5.5 m long and 1.5 m in the beam: buff sides flaring out from a narrow flat bottom, a strong sheer up to the bow and the narrow tombstone transom, green gunwales, three thwarts and a pair of oars inside, painter trailing from the bow.
