| dory_upturned | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor | A Banks dory hauled out and turned over on two skids for the winter, 5.5 m long and 1.5 m wide: buff flat bottom up, green gunwales down, bow to local -Z. Levels a little ground; carves nothing. |
| dovecote | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | A round stone dovecote 5.5 m across and 6 m to the eaves, with rows of nesting holes and ledges, a conical stone-slate roof, a timber lantern and a finial. 1 m x 2.5 m door with timber frame on local -Z; hollow inside, carves nothing. |
| drift_bell | bonebight | bonebight | A tethered drift-bell, drawn as a platform that rides the tide: a ribbed dome 1.6 m across and 1.2 m high over a frilled skirt and short tentacles, 2.25 m tall in all. Not placed by the chronicle; world.json's platforms draw it. |
| drift_log | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | A whole storm-thrown spruce about 18 m long, bleached silver, butt (1.1 m thick) to the west with a ragged fan of root stubs, a few snapped branch stubs and a split along the grain; it lies along x and sinks 0.75 m into the sand. |
| drift_log_b | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | A broken trunk section about 10 m long and 1.3 m thick lying along x, root swell to the west and a splintered snapped end to the east, sunk into the sand. |
| drift_log_c | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | Two spruce trunks jammed together: a 14 m trunk lying along x and a second, thinner one slanting across it and resting 1.4 m up; about 14.5 by 8 m. |
| drift_log_d | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | A low root wad: a 3 m root plate standing on edge facing west with a metre of trunk behind it, sunk into the sand. |
| drift_pile | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Driftwood piled where the storm left it: three trunks across each other at the foot of the dunes. |
| drift_shelter | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | A driftwood shelter: logs leaned against a ridge pole in a ring, the kind people build on this coast and leave. |
| drowned_church | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | St. Anne of the Marrow, the parish church that went down with the old lobe: a white board church, nave 9 x 15 m with lancet windows and a chancel behind (+Z), and a square tower on the front (local -Z), 5 m on a side, with the door, a louvred belfry and corner pinnacles 17.75 m up. | drowned_lights | lowmouth | lowmouth | Lamplight in a sunken_house: a lit attic window in its front gable just over the water, and the front windows lit under it. Place exactly over one (same spot, bearing and height). |
| drowned_stump | ribshore | ribshore | The stump of a spruce the sea drowned three hundred years ago: silver, round, flared at the root, snapped off jagged at chest height. |
| drowned_stump_tall | ribshore | ribshore | A drowned spruce that broke higher: a silver trunk three metres tall, flared at the root and splintered at the top. |
| drying_rack | windbarrow | thornpan, windbarrow | Three hides stretched to dry on an oak frame 7 m long, in the yards of Durn. Long axis local X, hides facing local -Z. |
| duck_blind | lowmouth | lowmouth | A duck blind out in the flats, 4.2 m tall: a 3 m by 2.5 m plank deck 0.5 m over the water on four posts, walled with bundled reeds to eye height, a firing slot on local -Z, a slot in each side, a bench, a reed roof over the front half and a plank gangplank behind. | duelbox | duelbox | duelbox | One empty room, written by worlds/duelbox/make_box.py: fifty-eight metres by thirty-four, a flat floor on a plinth, four walls, a roof and six lamps. Nothing to hide behind, which is the point -- it is a bench for weapons, not a map. Edit the script, not this file. |

| falls_terrace | saurischia | saurischia | A viewing terrace at the head of the falls: a limestone platform 16 m by 10 m with a 1.25 m balustrade on the front (-Z, the open view side) and both ends, two benches, and a round pavilion of eight columns and a small dome in the back-left corner. | ferry_landing | redrock | redrock | The ferry landing at the end of the highway, 12 m wide and 23 m long: an asphalt apron raised 1.25 m on a concrete fill, with a 2 by 3 m toll booth on an island under a steel canopy and a boom across the lane, a slope up from the road at the back (local +Z) under a sign gantry, and a concrete ramp 10 m wide running 8 m down toward the lake at the front (local -Z) between timber fender piles. | fort_ruin | lowmouth | lowmouth | Fort St. Jean, a brick half-moon fort 17.5 m across and 10 m deep: a curved wall 4.5 m high with gun embrasures and a banquette behind, facing the Gulf (local +Z), a straight 3.5 m back wall with pilasters and a 2 m by 2.5 m sally port on local -Z, two barracks, a vaulted magazine and one gun on its carriage. | free_arch | redrock | vermilion | A free-standing sandstone arch 20 m across and 18.5 m high on a slickrock bench, with legs 4 to 5 m thick and a span about 3.5 m thick under an uneven top. | gambrel_barn | gannet | unused | Red gambrel barn, 12 m wide by 18 m long and 11 m to the ridge, front (local -Z) a pair of 5 m sliding doors half open on a rail with a hay door, hood and hoist beam high in the gambrel and a cupola on the ridge; inside a drive floor between stalls, with a hayloft over both sides. | gannet_light | ribshore | ribshore | The Gannet Head light: a white tower 27 m tall on a stepped basalt and fieldstone plinth, tapering, banded three times in black, stair windows climbing round it in recesses with stone sills and lintels, a gabled porch with a transomed door up a flight of steps between cheek walls with iron rails, a corbelled gallery on twelve brackets with a railing, a glazed lantern, and a domed cap with a vent, a ball and a lightning rod, 35 m to its tip. | generator_shed | saltmirror | gannet, saltmirror | The camp's generator shed, 6.5 m by 4.5 m on a concrete pad: a corrugated tin shed with its sliding doors gone from a 3 m by 2.5 m front opening (local -Z), a red diesel set inside with its exhaust through the roof and a fuel tank rusting on cradles behind (local +Z). |
| georgian_house | gannet | hemlow | A five-bay Georgian house 13 m wide by 10 m deep, two storeys and an attic under a side-gabled roof, white clapboard on a granite plinth with a pedimented doorway, and a double-flued brick chimney at each gable end. Faces local -Z: centred hall running through with a stair at the back, a room each side of it front and rear on both floors with fireplaces on the end walls; carves nothing. |
| ghost_steamboat | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | A sternwheel paddle steamer, 31 m long with the wheel, 7.5 m in the beam and 16 m to the stack tops: the Belle Marrow, lost in the flood of the old century. Hull with a spoon bow, an open freight deck and engine room, a boiler-deck saloon with staterooms, the texas and pilothouse above, two black stacks and a red paddle wheel at the stern; lamps in every window. Doors are 1 m by 2 m, stairs lead up the bow and both sides. Bow to local -Z. Base sea. |
| giant_harpoon | ribshore | ribshore | A harpoon about thirty metres long, an iron shaft 1.5 m thick leaning down from a butt 12 m up to a barbed head buried in the bone, rusted through in places, with an eye at the butt and a chain twelve metres long hanging to the sand. | glass_spire | glassfall | glassfall | A needle of skyglass fifty metres tall, leaning slightly, with a foot some five metres thick, ringed by seven great crystals and sixteen shards on a melted skirt. A narrow crack on local -Z, one metre wide, opens into a hollow throat fifteen metres tall. |
| granary | windbarrow | hemlow, saurischia, thornpan, windbarrow | A Windbarrow granary: a daub store on four oak legs with fieldstone caps against rats, 4 m wide under a straw cone and over 8 m tall, the floor 2.75 m off the ground. | harlow_smithy | redrock | redrock | Harlow's smithy, 8 m wide with its lean-to and 6.5 m deep, the front open to the street: plank back and side walls under a tin gable roof, a brick forge with a hood and a 3.5 m chimney, bellows, an anvil on a stump, a quench trough, and and an open lean-to for wood and coal. | headframe | redrock | redrock | The timber headframe over Harlow's main shaft: a 4.5 m square tower rising 15 m above its deck, X-braced in four bays, with a spoked 2.75 m sheave wheel on top and a 4 m square hoist house behind it that the back legs land on. | hemlow_smithy | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | Hemlow's smithy: 7 m by 6 m walls of honey stone under a stone-slate roof, 6.5 m to the ridge (8 m by 6.8 m over the eaves) and a front open for 5 m under an oak lintel. | herb_beds | - | hemlow | Three raised herb beds edged with boards: green herbs among pink-flowered roses. |
| hob_stones | - | hemlow | The Hob Stones: a ring of eleven knobbly standing stones, 2-4.5 m tall and 14 m across, with glowing moss in cups on some faces, a paved hearth stone at the middle, two fallen stones, and a taller leaning King Stone 5 m high outside the ring to the north-west (local -X, -Z). | long_barrow_mouth | windbarrow | hemlow, windbarrow | The open east end of the Long Barrow: a kerbed forecourt with ochre on its floor, two uprights and a lintel at the mouth, the closing slab pushed aside and lying, and a chalk-lined passage carved 12 m back into the mound. Place at the mound's east end facing out (local -Z); it carves the passage into the earth behind it. |
| longhouse | windbarrow | windbarrow | The longhouse where a Windbarrow village gathers: 14 m by 7 m and 10.2 m to its ridge, daub walls on a fieldstone course between oak posts, two rows of aisle posts, a stone hearth and a wattle partition under a steep straw roof, with a smoke gap in the ridge. Two doors, each 1.5 m by 2.5 m, in the front wall (local -Z). |
| mansard_block | saurischia | unused | A Paris-style block 16 m wide by 12 m deep: a ground floor of tall arched shop openings with a 6 m carriage arch in the middle, three storeys of pale stone with iron balconies on the second and top, and a steep grey slate mansard with dormers and four chimney stacks. Faces local -Z; the carriage arch runs through to the back, shops either side, carves nothing. |
| market_cross | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The market cross on Hemlow green, a 4 x 4 m base rising 7 m: three 0.25 m steps, a square plinth, a shaft with a collar, and a ringed cross head. No front; it reads the same from every side. |
| market_hall | - | hemlow | Hemlow's market hall, 14.5 m by 6.75 m: an open ground floor of five arched bays by two on square stone piers over flagstones, a wool room above lit by three-light mullioned windows, under a steep stone-slate roof with coped gables 10.25 m to the ridge. Front (a long side) faces local -Z; carves nothing. |
| market_spruce | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | The market's Christmas tree: a tall spruce hung with warm lights and red baubles, a gold star on top. |
| mb_house | kitehold | kitehold | Maple-timber cottage 6 m by 5 m with a shingle roof, front (local -Z) door 1.25 m by 2.5 m with a cobble step, and a cobble chimney on the back gable. It carves nothing. |
| mb_inn | kitehold | kitehold | Two-storey maple-timber inn 12 m by 8 m on a river-cobble footing, front (local -Z) door 1.5 m by 2.5 m behind a 2 m deep porch with benches, a sign bracket, a cobble chimney on the +X end, a common room below and three bedrooms above. It carves nothing. |
| mb_mill | kitehold | kitehold | Two-storey maple-timber mill 8 m by 7 m on a cobble ground floor, front (local -Z) door 1.25 m by 2.5 m, millstones in tuns inside, and a 5 m overshot wheel on the +X side fed by a 10 m timber flume on trestles. It carves nothing. |
| mb_toll_bridge | kitehold | kitehold | Covered timber toll bridge, 26 m long with 4.5 m between maple-plank walls, on river-cobble abutments, with a band of small windows, a shingle gable roof, a gabled portal at each end and a 2.5 m by 2.5 m toll booth beside the -Z portal. Long axis along local Z; base rim with the anchor on the deck row at mid-span, carves nothing. |
| mess_hall | saltmirror | saltmirror | The camp's mess hall (1966): a 24 by 13 m prefab with a flat tin roof, 5.8 m to its stovepipe, a row of windows and a porch with the ajar yellow door on the front (local -Z), trestle tables inside and a kitchen counter with a stovepipe at the back. |
| mill | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, kitehold_rock | Stone mill 6 m by 6.5 m, walls 3 m with a loft, front (local -Z) door 1 m by 2.75 m, a granite millstone inside, and a 4.25 m-radius undershot wheel on the +X side driven through an iron axle. |
| mine_portal | redrock | glassfall, redrock | The timbered mouth of a Harlow adit: a double timber frame with knee braces and a plank face, a spoil heap and an ore cart outside, narrow-gauge rails running in. Faces -Z; carves a tunnel 17 m long, 3 m wide with a 2.5 m ceiling, straight back into the hill, with timber sets near the mouth. Place at the foot of a slope or cliff. |
| miner_shack | redrock | glassfall, redrock | A one-room miner's shack, 4 m by 5.5 m with 2.75 m walls, plank walls, a steep tin gable roof with 0.5 m eaves, a small stoop, a 1 m by 2.5 m front door (open leaf inside), three windows, a bunk, a table and an iron stove with a pipe through the roof. Front is -Z; carves nothing. |
| mire_chapel | lowmouth | lowmouth | A timber chapel 20 voxels (5 m) wide and 32 deep (8 m) on brick piers, 4 m to the eaves, with a small gabled stoop, double doors 1.5 m by 2.5 m, tall side windows, pews and a chancel step inside, and an open bell gable on the front ridge. Faces local -Z; nothing is carved. |
| mire_school | lowmouth | lowmouth | A one-room schoolhouse 20 voxels (5 m) wide and 24 deep (6 m) on brick piers, front-gabled over a 2 m deep porch, with double doors 1.5 m by 2.25 m, four tall windows a side, rows of desks, a blackboard on the back wall and a stove with a pipe through the roof. Faces local -Z; nothing is carved. |
| mokoro | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | A mokoro: a dugout canoe six metres long, drawn up on the sand with its pole laid along the thwart. Place in twos and threes at a landing, bow to the water. |
| mossy_log | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, saurischia, windbarrow | An old log, long down and sinking into the duff, moss along its top. |
| mussel_bed | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold, ribshore | A bed of California mussels packed over two low rock ridges at the waterline, about 5 by 3.5 m and up to a metre above the rock, with gooseneck barnacles in the crack between the ridges and rockweed at the edges. |
| narrow_house | saurischia | unused | A thin infill house 4.5 m wide and 11 m deep, four storeys of rose stucco with one window on each floor above a door and a ground-floor window, under a front-gabled terracotta roof and a tall chimney. The front faces local -Z; the side walls are plain party walls, and a rear door and window mirror the front. |
| naust | kaldvik | kaldvik | A boathouse 10.5 m long and 6 m wide under a turf roof, with a 3 m wide open boat door in the end that faces the water (local -X), a man door in the -Z wall and a clinker boat on the slip. It levels the ground. |
| net_shed | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, lowmouth, ribshore, thornpan | An open-fronted net shed on posts over the water: 4 m by 5 m, 2.5 m above the bed, nets drying on a rail along its front (local -Z) on a 1 m apron. Base sea; the front wall is carved open. |
| ochre_house | kaldvik | kaldvik | A one-storey yellow-ochre cottage, 8 m by 7 m with its porch, on a fieldstone plinth under a turf roof, with a stone chimney and a small gabled porch off-centre on the front. Faces local -Z; door on the porch, carves nothing. |
| offering_post | windbarrow | windbarrow | An oak post 4 m tall at a parting of the trails, a horse skull set on its top facing local -Z and strips of hide and ochred cloth tied below it. |
| oil_platform | lowmouth | lowmouth | A small oil well platform out in the Gulf: a steel deck 16 m square on four cross-braced piles, 6.5 m over the water, a boat landing and ladder on local -Z, a wellhead over its conductor pipe, a tank, a shed and a 19 m lattice derrick with a light on top. Base sea; the piles and the conductor run down to the bed. |
| optical_dome | saltmirror | saltmirror | Ilse Varga's observatory (1959) on an 11 by 13 m pad: a concrete drum under a white dome, 7.9 m to its top, with the rusted shutter leaf half closed over the slit on the front (local -Z), a refractor on a pier inside and an instrument hut with a roof mast behind; the door is on the front and it carves nothing. |
| ore_cart | redrock | glassfall, redrock | A tipped-over ore cart, rusted to the colour of the ground. |
| packhorse_bridge | - | hemlow | A medieval packhorse bridge: three ringed arches on cutwatered piers, a 1.5 m cobbled way humped 0.5 m over the middle, 1 m parapets with coping and two passing bays. 16 m long by 4 m wide along local X; the cutwaters point up and down stream (local -Z and +Z), so place it with X across the stream and it carves nothing. |
| pagoda | jadewater | jadewater | A three-storey pagoda, 6.5 m square at its widest eave and 15.8 m to the top of its lacquered finial, storeys 3 m tall with railed balconies on the upper two. Door 1 m by 2.75 m on local -Z, windows on the upper floors; carves nothing. |
| pavilion | jadewater | jadewater | An open pavilion of the Order, 5 m square: four lacquered posts on a stone footing with a rail on three sides, under a tiled gable roof 5 m across, 6.5 m to the ridge. Open front on local -Z; settles on its lowest corner and the footing fills down. |
| petrified_log | redrock | redrock | A petrified log lying on the clay, broken into rounds where it weathered out. |
| pg_buoy | bonebight | bonebight | A faded orange marker buoy 2.5 m across with a gold band, a rust-streaked lattice mast and a dead lamp, 5 m tall overall. The waterline is 1 m above the base, so the iron keel is under water. |
| pg_calypso | bonebight | bonebight | Station Calypso: the maroon rim capsule, 11.7 m by 11.8 m and 24.9 m to the crane top, with pale glass crust over the weed band and glass spikes up its east and north sides and over the roof, and its crane snapped. The walkway door and landing are at 4.5 m on the +Z side. |
| pg_ferry_house | bonebight | bonebight | The ferry shelter on the quay, 8 m by 6 m in concrete with a harvest-gold stripe and a rounded orange barrel-vault roof 4 m high at the crown. It is open on local -Z; inside are a bench and a timetable board on the back wall, and each side wall has a round porthole. |
| pg_station | bonebight | bonebight | Rim station: a maroon-brown steel capsule, 11.6 m by 11.6 m with its stairs and 24.9 m to the crane top, six floors of 3 m with a spiral stair, a wet lab at the waterline, balconies and zigzag stairs outside and a jib crane on the roof. A door and landing at 4.5 m on the +Z side take the walkway. |
| pg_station_dome | bonebight | bonebight | Dive station: an avocado steel capsule, 12.4 m by 12.4 m with its stairs and 24.6 m to the dome mast, six floors with a spiral stair, topped by an 8 m orange geodesic dome and a davit hanging a dive cage on the east side. The walkway door and landing are at 4.5 m on the +Z side. |
| pg_station_main | bonebight | bonebight | Base station: an orange steel capsule, 15.6 m by 15.9 m with its annex and 27.2 m to the mast top, with a wet lab and moon pool at the bottom, floors every 3 m with a spiral stair, a radar room with amber screens at the top, a dish on the roof, an annex capsule and a landing stage on the west side. The walkway door and landing are at 4.5 m on the +Z side. |
| pg_submersible | bonebight | bonebight | A yellow research submarine 7 m long and 3 m across, moored afloat with its bow (the acrylic port) toward +X, a gold hull with teal and orange stripes, a conning sail with an open hatch, two thrusters and skids; the waterline is at y 4 to 6 and it carves nothing. |
| pickup_truck | saltmirror | saltmirror | A 1970s pickup left where it was parked ten years ago: long bonnet, a bench-seat cab, an open bed with nothing in it, tyres flat and the paint faded and rusting through. Front faces local -Z. The paint is the `paint` slot. |
| pinky | gannet | bonebight, gannet | A pinky, the small pink-sterned fishing schooner of the bay, 13 m on deck and 3.8 m in the beam: slate-blue hull over a red bottom, white rail, the bulwarks carried aft into the high narrow pink with the tiller through it, two gaff-rigged masts with the sails furled on the booms, a short bowsprit, a cuddy forward with a stovepipe, the fish hold hatch amidships and barrels on deck. Bow to local -Z. The anchor is the waterline: place her with `"height": 0` for her 1.75 m draft. |
| plumose_anemones | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Plumose anemones on the deep bones and the sea floor: white stalks a metre and a half tall with feathery crowns. |
| prefab_bungalow | saltmirror | saltmirror | A flat-roofed prefab bungalow for the married staff (1970), a 10 m by 5.5 m teal-sided box 3 m to the eaves on an 11 m by 7 m slab, with a covered porch, green door and two windows on the front (local -Z), a blue back door, an air-conditioner on the back slab, and two rooms inside. It carves nothing. |
| pump_station | lowmouth | lowmouth | A concrete pump house 7 m wide and 6 m deep with a 3.5 m interior, facing local -Z; the 1.5 m by 2.5 m steel-framed door has a hoist beam over it, and two 1 m rusted pipes run out its back (local +Z) on piers over the bank. It carves nothing and sits on a levelled pad. |
| quarry_hut | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow | Plank tool shed 4 m square with 2.75 m walls, a 1 m by 2.5 m door at the front (local -Z), three small windows, a work bench and a tool rack on the back wall. |
| quonset_hut | saltmirror | gannet, saltmirror | The camp's quonset hut, a half-round corrugated hut 6 m wide, 9 m long and 3.5 m to the crown on a concrete slab, with end walls, an open door (1 m by 2.25 m) and two windows on the front end (local -Z), a window at the back, benches and a stack of drums inside. It carves nothing. |
| race_deck | kestrel | unused | A piece of a circuit's deck: road on a slab with kerbs and parapets, on pillars, where the road stands over the ground or crosses over itself. Its voxels are built by the `circuit` event; this entry only names the placement. `base: rim` keeps it where the event put it. |
| race_gate | kestrel | unused | The gantry over a circuit's start/finish line: two posts outside the kerbs and a chequered beam across. Its voxels are built by the `circuit` event to fit the road where the line is; this entry only names the placement. `base: rim` keeps it where the event put it. |
| race_tunnel | vermilion | unused | A piece of a race track's tunnel: the hollow bored over the road, the road on its floor and a metre of lining round it (track.json `tunnels`). | radio_shack | saltmirror | gannet, saltmirror | The camp's radio shack, a 5 m by 4 m clad hut 2.5 m to the eaves on a 7 m by 6.5 m slab, with its open door (1 m by 2.25 m) and a window on the front (local -Z), a desk and rack of sets inside, and a 6 m antenna mast at the back corner held by three guy wires. It carves nothing. |
| raised_tombs | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | A row of four whitewashed tombs built above the ground (the dead can't be buried in ground this wet), each with a gabled top and an iron cross. Fronts face local -Z. |
| red_boulder | redrock | redrock, vermilion | A rounded sandstone boulder fallen from the cliffs. |
| red_house | kaldvik | kaldvik | A two-storey Falu-red house, 9.5 m by 8 m with its porch, 3 m storeys, under a slate roof with a front cross-gable, a small porch hood over the centred door and a chimney at each end. Faces local -Z; door under the hood, back door opposite, carves nothing. |
| ring_wall | thornpan | thornpan | The Ringstone: a dry-stone enclosure fifteen metres across, coursed and fitted without mortar, with one gap and a paved middle. Built by people who were gone before the present ones came. Place it on a kopje shoulder, gap downhill. |
| road_barrier | redrock | redrock | A striped barricade and a ROAD CLOSED board where a road ends. |
| rock_outcrop | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, kitehold, peregrine, redrock, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | A tor: weathered blocks of rock stacked on each other. |
| rope_bridge_24 | jadewater | jadewater | The Order's lacquered rope bridge, 24 m along local X and 3 m wide, a 2 m plank deck with gaps between the boards sagging 1.00 m at mid-span, two main cables, hand ropes 1.1 m up on hangers every 2 m, and a red gate 2.25 m clear at each end. | rope_walk | gannet | gannet | The town rope walk: a one-storey shingled shed 6 m wide and 150 m long along local Z under a low gable roof, a two-storey head house at the -Z end with the door on that front, and rope-making frames with hooks every 10 m in the long room. Small windows every 3 m on both sides. |
| rorbu | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | A fisherman's rorbu, 8.5 x 8.5 m: a Falu-red cabin 8 x 6 m on stilts 2.5 m above the water, with a 2 m front jetty carrying a drying rack, hoist, casks and a ladder. Door on local -Z; carves nothing. |
| rotunda | saurischia | saurischia | The domed hall of Cascadelle: a pale-stone drum 28 m across with 20 columns round it, four arched entrances 7 m across and 11 m clear on the compass points (front is -Z), an open floor inside with a dark-stone long-neck on a plinth in the middle, its head toward the front door, and a shell dome with a glazed lantern, 31 m to the finial. It carves its interior. |
| round_house | windbarrow | thornpan, windbarrow | A Windbarrow round house 7.5 m wide and 8.8 m tall: a daub wall ring of 6 m on a fieldstone footing, oak posts and rafters, a stone-ringed hearth, a deep straw cone with a smoke hole. The door, 1 m by 2.75 m, is on local -Z between two wattle screens. |
| round_house_small | windbarrow | thornpan, windbarrow | A small Windbarrow round house 6 m wide and 7.5 m to its ridge: a 4.5 m daub wall ring on a fieldstone footing under a straw cone, with a stone hearth and a smoke hole. The door, 1 m by 2.5 m, is on local -Z between two wattle screens. |
| ruined_tower | glassfall | glassfall | A round fieldstone watchtower 8 m across at its battered foot and 22.5 m to its parapet, its crown broken away on the +X+Z side down to 14 m and the fallen stones heaped below; door on local -Z under a slate lintel, a cantilevered stone stair climbing the inside past slit windows to the wall walk. It carves nothing. |
| sail_loft | gannet | gannet_harbor | A sail loft: 8 m by 24 m, two storeys of grey clapboard under a brown shingle gable, front local -Z with a wide storage door; the whole upper floor is one open loft with a window row down both long sides, an outside stair up the east wall to the loft door, and canvas laid out on the floor. Carves its interior. |
| saloon | redrock | redrock | A Harlow saloon, 7 m wide and 12 m deep: plank walls, a hidden tin gable roof behind a 9 m false front with a sign board, a 2 m boardwalk with a railed balcony and tin roof over it, a 1.5 m swing-door opening, a long bar, and a stair up to two rooms. Front is -Z; carves nothing. |
| saltbox | gannet | gannet_harbor | A saltbox house 11 m wide by 10 m deep, two storeys at the front with five windows across the upper floor, a short steep front slope to a ridge 2 m back and a long rear slope falling 2 in per 1 to a lean-to eave at 2.75 m, with a central brick chimney. Faces local -Z: centred door, stair hall, a room each side of the chimney, a kitchen lean-to under the rear roof and an attic reached by a stair from the west upper room; carves nothing. |
| sauropod_gate | saurischia | saurischia | The triumphal city gate: a limestone block 18 m wide and 6 m deep, 19 m to the parapet, with one great arch 9 m wide and 13 m to its crown, and a 3 m pedestrian arch each side, pilasters, a cornice and an attic. Its axis runs along local Z (walk through it front to back); it carves the three passages. |
| sea_palms | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | Sea palms on the surf-beaten rock: rubbery stalks a little under a metre tall, each with a mop of blades. |
| sea_stars | ribshore | bonebight, ribshore | Ochre sea stars in a cluster on the rock at the waterline, and one sunflower star a metre and a half across. |
| sea_torii | jadewater | jadewater | A great torii standing in the sea, its feet in the water, 5.5 m tall. Base sea; the posts fill down to the sea floor. Front local -Z. |
| settler_barn | glassfall | glassfall | Timber barn, 7 m by 9 m under a thatched roof with a hayloft over the rear half, a 3 m by 2.5 m cart door on the front (local -Z) with one leaf slid shut, and a hay hatch with hoist beam in the gable. Carves its cart door, hatch and wall vents. |
| settler_house | glassfall | glassfall, redrock | One-room fieldstone cottage, 6 m by 7 m under a thatched roof with 0.25 m eaves, glazed shuttered windows, a 1 m by 2.25 m plank door and a chimney outside the east end; door on the front (local -Z). Carves its door, windows and firebox. |
| settler_longhouse | glassfall | glassfall | Long stone house, 10 m by 6 m under a slate roof, with two plank doors on the front (local -Z): a family room with glazed windows and a chimney on the west end, and a byre with a 1.25 m door. Carves its doors, windows and the partition gap. |
| settler_school | glassfall | glassfall | One-room school, 6 m by 7 m under a slate roof, with a 1 m by 2.5 m plank door on the front (local -Z), tall glazed windows, a stove, rows of desks and a bell cote on the front ridge. Carves its door and windows. |
| settler_smithy | glassfall | glassfall | Stone forge, 7 m by 7 m under a slate roof, with an open-fronted work bay on the front (local -Z), a plank door to the store room beside it, and a hearth with a stone chimney against the back wall. Carves the bay, door, windows and firebox. |
| settler_store | glassfall | glassfall | General store, 6.5 m by 7 m under a slate roof, with a 2 m deep front porch with railings, a 1 m by 2.5 m plank door, glazed shop windows and a sign board over the porch; door on the front (local -Z). Carves its door and windows. |
| shotgun_double | lowmouth | lowmouth | A two-family shotgun house 25 voxels (6.25 m) wide and 26 deep (6.5 m) on brick piers, front-gabled over a shared 2 m deep gallery, with two 1 m by 2.25 m doors side by side, a party wall down the middle and rooms in a row on each side. Faces local -Z; nothing is carved. |
| shrimp_boat_wreck | lowmouth | lowmouth | A shrimp trawler sunk at her mooring, 15 m long and 4.5 m in the beam, settled in the mud so the deck is awash: a curved hull with a raised bow, a torn wheelhouse you can enter by the door in its aft wall (1 m by 2 m), a stove-in port bilge, a collapsed aft deck, a half-open fish hold and a leaning iron mast with one boom fallen. Bow to local -Z. Base sea. |
| shrine_ruin | jadewater | jadewater | A shrine platform 8 m square with a 4 m stair, four pillars broken at 2 to 5 m, a fallen shaft and a stone lantern at the middle. Front (stair) local -Z; level ground. |
| sign_pole_tall | redrock | redrock, saltmirror | Two steel legs holding a big board over a highway shoulder. They run up behind the board to just under its top, the way a real gantry sign is built, so no gap shows under it. The board is drawn as plates (world/places.rs), not voxels. |
| sign_post_single | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow, lowmouth, redrock, saltmirror | One squared timber post with a small board on top: a trail sign or a finger post. The board is drawn as plates (world/places.rs), not voxels. |
| sign_post_steel | city | gannet_harbor | A galvanised pole for a street blade or a road sign, 2.9 m to the top. The blade is drawn as plates (world/places.rs), not voxels. |
| sign_posts_timber | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, ribshore, saurischia | Two squared oak posts 25 cm thick and 2.25 m tall, set 1.75 m apart so a 2.4 m name board nailed across their faces hides them at its ends; a village notice or a name board at the edge of a settlement. | annas_hummingbird | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | hum: Anna's hummingbird, a male: bronze-green back, grey belly, and a gorget and crown of rose-magenta that look near black until the sun catches them, when they flash (the gorget's fourth colour value below zero is iridescence, scene.wgsl). Sits on a bare twig at the top of a shrub, darts out to hover at flowers -- lupine, ocotillo in spring, desert willow, meadow wildflowers -- and now and then climbs high and dives in a narrow U, again and again, the way it courts. | boar_juvenile | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The boar's juvenile form (boar `forms`): a piglet, red-brown with cream stripes running the length of its body and no bristly mane yet. Only its model is used. |
| bone_crab | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | wade: A crab far larger than any living one, near three metres across the legs: a deep rust shell studded with pale knobs over slate-blue legs, one great crushing claw and a small one, on short eyestalks. Scuttles sideways over the shore and the shallows, picking at whatever the tide leaves, and settles flat on the sand to rest. Walks to its right: the model is drawn with that side at -Z. |
| bot | shooter | unused | The class root of the shooter's and the kart races' bots, on the humanoid frame (eighteen bones, 18.5 mm voxels). It holds the shared paint and the plain android body that the three builds (bot_courier, bot_hardsuit, bot_walker) replace or keep. The shooter paints the uniform colour in the side's colour and draws a lit bar across the chest on the dark band (front and back, rows 74 to 78), so plates stay near white and the band stays clear of the arms. |
| bot_courier | shooter | unused | Slim smooth android: egg head with a wraparound visor band, capsule limbs, a small pelvis and a few seam lines. It is the bot's own body, close to the humanoid's proportions. |
| bot_hardsuit | shooter | unused | An atmosphere hardsuit: big round helmet with a porthole, bulky limbs with ribbed bands, heavy boots and twin tanks behind the shoulders. The arm joints sit wider than the humanoid's. |
| bot_walker | shooter | unused | Reverse-knee walker: the thigh runs forward and down to a knee in front of the body, the shin back and down to an ankle 0.35 m up, and a long metatarsal to a three-toed foot. Torso pitched forward, long arms, a narrow sensor head with a crest. Modelled standing at rest; the knees bend the other way. |
| brachiosaurus | dinosaurs | saurischia | graze: Brachiosaurus: a sauropod of the high-browsing kind, 22 m long and over 12 m to the top of its head. The fore legs are longer than the hind, so the back slopes down from tall shoulders, and the neck rises steeply to a small head with a high arch over the nostrils. Warm grey-brown, mottled, paler beneath. Moves in small herds, slowly, and browses tree tops; it hardly minds anyone. |
| brown_pelican | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, ribshore, ribstrand | circle: Brown pelican: big and grey-brown, flying low over the bay and the sound and floating on it. |
| bullfrog | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Bullfrog: twice the frog and olive-dark, sitting heavy in the shallows of a swamp or a slow river. |
| bumblebee | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, windbarrow | Bumblebee: big, round, furry, black and yellow, slow from flower to flower in cooler, damper places. |
| butterflyfish | underwater | gannet, glassfall, jadewater | school: Butterflyfish: a yellow disc of a fish about 20 cm long with a dark band through the eye, in ones and twos picking over the coral. The base of the deep-bodied reef fish. v2: one flat disc body with a snout, separate dorsal and anal fins, a peduncle and forked tail, and eyes set into the band. |
| canyon_bat | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Canyon bat: the smallest of them, pale sandy over a black face, out early in the evening along the rims and over the pools. |
| capybara | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | wade: Capybara: the biggest rodent, a russet barrel with a blunt square head and no tail. Families graze the river banks and slip into the water rather than run far; very calm about people. v2: rebuilt from few large overlapping ellipsoids and segment legs for the decimated bake. |
| cave_bear | windbarrow | windbarrow | graze: Cave bear: half again the bulk of a black bear, dark brown with a high shoulder hump and a pale muzzle, denning in the chalk caves and out at the mouths at dawn and dusk. |
| channel_catfish | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Channel catfish: grey-olive, whiskered, in loose groups along the bayous and the channels. |
| cod | underwater | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | Atlantic cod: olive and speckled, hanging over the kelp floor in the cold water. |
| compsognathus | dinosaurs | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, saurischia | skulk: Compsognathus: a little dinosaur a metre long, most of it tail, and no higher than a hen; a long neck, a small head and a coat of short fuzz. Olive-grey with a dark stripe down the back, pale beneath. Runs about in small parties after lizards and anything else it can catch. Version 2 redraws it at 15 mm voxels from a few large overlapping shapes and capsule limbs. |
| condor | redrock | kitehold, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | soar: California condor: black, with a bare pink-grey head and white bands under the wings, 3 m across. Soars for hours on the cliff updrafts, alone or in pairs, and hardly beats its wings. |
| cormorant | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, ribshore, ribstrand | circle: Pelagic cormorant: glossy black-brown, thick-necked, with a short hooked bill and a bare yellow-orange throat patch, flying low and straight over the water and sitting on it between dives. |
| cougar | base | bonebight, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | skulk: Cougar: a long, low tan cat with a black-tipped tail, alone and rarely seen. It hunts deer and keeps well away from people. v3 is drawn from capsule legs, a long hanging tail and flattened ellipsoid paws, ears and muzzle, so it decimates without bricks. |
| cougar_juvenile | base | bonebight, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The cougar's juvenile form (cougar `forms`): a kitten, paler than its mother with dark brown spots on the flanks and legs and dark rings round the tail, which it loses in its first year. Only its model is used. |
| cow | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, windbarrow | Dairy cow: the bison's frame with the hump shaved off and a straight back, brown over white, standing about the pasture in twos and threes. Pays people no mind. |
| cow_female | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, windbarrow | The cow's female form (cow `forms`): a cow: lighter than the bull, with thinner horns. Only its model is used; it spawns as a cow. Written once from the cow's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| cow_juvenile | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, windbarrow | The cow's juvenile form (cow `forms`): a calf, the female's body without her horns, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| coyote | redrock | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | skulk: Coyote: grey-tan with a darker saddle, pale throat and belly, narrow dark-nosed muzzle, big upright ears, long slim legs and a bushy tail carried low with a dark tip; hunts jackrabbits and lizards, keeps its distance. v3: rebuilt at 0.0275 m voxels on a 64^3 grid; the hind legs hinge at the stifle. v4: 0.0283 m voxels on a 32x44x60 grid, fewer larger shapes and round ears and feet so it decimates cleanly. v4: 0.0283 m voxels on a 32x44x60 grid, fewer larger shapes and round ears and feet so it decimates cleanly. |
| crocodile | lowmouth | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | haulout: American crocodile, grey-olive and narrow-snouted, in the brackish water of the river mouth: hauled out on the mud banks in the sun, it watches you come and slides into the water before you are close. In the water it lies awash with only its back, eyes and snout out, and sinks away when you come close. The base of the delta's crocodilians. Long flat snout, ridge of dark scutes down the back and tail, eyes raised on the skull. |
| crow | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | circle: Crows wheel over open burned ground. |
| crystal_bat | base | hemlow, jadewater | Crystal bat: wings like lit glass, ringing the cenotes and the lantern caves in the dark. |
| deer | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | graze: Red deer on a 0.0467 m voxel grid, modelled for decimation: one long barrel with a deep chest and haunch blended into it, slender muzzle with a dark nose and pale chin, large ears, long tapering neck, thin legs with darker lower legs, knees, hocks and rounded dark hooves, a pale rump patch and a short tail with a white underside. Usually in small family groups that graze together and bolt together. |
| deer_female | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The deer's female form (deer `forms`): a doe: smaller than the buck and without antlers. | deer_juvenile | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The deer's juvenile form (deer `forms`): a spotted fawn, the hornless female's body with white spots in rows down the back and flanks, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. | desert_toad | base | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Desert toad: sand-coloured under dark blotches, out at the canyon pools and the wet sand after rain. |
| desert_tortoise | redrock | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Desert tortoise: smaller and sandier than its parent, with a taller, narrower domed shell plated by recolour, a rounded head with dark eyes, and stout scaly legs; spends the heat of the day in a burrow and the morning cropping desert plants. |
| diplodocus | dinosaurs | saurischia | graze: Diplodocus: the long, low sauropod, 25 m from nose to the tip of a whip tail and under 4 m at the hips. The neck is held out level, the back rises to the hips and a row of small spines runs down it. Ochre with dark brown saddles across the back, neck and tail, pale beneath. Grazes low in loose herds, sweeping its head over the ground. |
| dipper | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | Dipper: a dumpy dark brown bird with a white bib that bobs on the stones of fast water and walks in under it. On the Hem, the Wend and the Fleece Brook. |
| donkey | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, redrock, saltmirror, vermilion | Feral burro: the miners' donkeys, turned loose when Harlow emptied. Grey-brown with a pale muzzle and belly, long ears, a short upright mane and a dark cross on the shoulders. Small herds round the old town; curious, slow to run. |
| douglas_squirrel | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, redrock, ribshore, vermilion | Douglas squirrel: a small dark olive squirrel with a rusty orange front, loud in the firs and gone round the trunk in a blink. |
| dragonfly | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | dart: Blue darner: a long blue dragonfly that holds still over the water and darts from spot to spot along the river and the shore. The base of every dragonfly; drawn a little larger than life. |
| dusk_wren | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | A small jewel-coloured wren with a softly glowing crown. Flits between shrubs in small groups. |
| eagle | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | soar: A bald eagle: white head and tail, dark brown body and wings, a heavy yellow hooked bill, circling high and alone over the slopes and the coast. Now and then it sits a while on the top of the tallest tree near. When it is hungry it stoops on what it eats in the open below it, a gull in the air or on the sea, a duck, a rabbit, a fish near the surface, and takes it or misses; a kill goes off in its talons, to a tree top if there is one. |
| eider | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | Common eiders rafting in the open water off the ice: the drakes white-backed and black-bellied with a green nape, the ducks barred brown. |
| eider_female | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | The eider's female form (eider `forms`): the duck, warm brown barred darker across the body, with the eider's long sloping bill. Only its model is used; it spawns as an eider. |
| elephant | base | gannet, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | graze: Elephant: three metres at the shoulder on four grey columns, with great ears, short tusks and a trunk that reaches the ground. Moves in small families and is in no hurry about anyone. |
| elephant_juvenile | base | gannet, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | The elephant's juvenile form (elephant `forms`): a calf with no tusks showing yet, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. | elk | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand | Elk: heavier and paler than the red deer, tan body and rump, a thick dark neck under a shaggy mane and a wide branching rack. | elk_female | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand | The elk's female form (elk `forms`): a cow: smaller than the bull and without antlers. | elk_juvenile | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand | The elk's juvenile form (elk `forms`): a spotted calf, the hornless female's body with white spots in rows down the back and flanks, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| ember_drake | kitehold | kitehold, saurischia | circle: Ember drake: a small soot-black pterosaur of the lava tubes, 2 m across the wings, with glowing orange veins in its wing membranes and a glowing throat and belly. It circles low over the lava field and is warm and curious, not wary of people. The pteranodon's model at under a third of the size. |
| fae_badger | base | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's badger: bigger and bolder, its grey gone moss-green, the stripes on its head glowing pale at dusk, with toadstools riding on its back. |
| fae_elephant | base | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's elephant: no taller than a pony, pale lilac and mossed along its back, its small tusks glowing at dusk. It lets children walk up to it. |
| fae_horse | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's horse: smaller and finer, a soft lilac coat, with a mane and tail that glow violet at dusk. Walks the glades in pairs and lets you come close. |
| fae_toad | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, lowmouth, saurischia | The faerie wood's toad: moss-backed and slow, with pale spots that glow at dusk. It sits where it sits and lets you look at it. |
| faerie_heron | faerie | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, lowmouth | A heron of the faerie wood: white with a trailing plume that glows pale violet, and wings that leave a shimmer as it lifts off. |
| firefly | - | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | flutter: Firefly: a small dark beetle whose tail lights green-gold in slow flashes, each on its own rhythm, drifting low over the long grass and the wood's edge at dusk. Drawn well over life size so it shows. |
| flamingo | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | wade: Greater flamingo: pale pink with coral wings edged black, on legs longer than its body. Stands in flocks in the brine at the low end of the pan, heads down, sieving for shrimp, and lifts off all at once in a pink sheet when you come too near. |
| fox | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, peregrine, saurischia, windbarrow | skulk: Red fox; hunts rabbits. Built at 25 mm voxels from a few overlapping shapes (deep chest, narrow waist, brush tail with a white tip, black socks and ear tips) for the decimated mesh. |
| frog | base | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | splash: Green frog: sits at the water edge with its eyes up over the surface and hops in when you come near. Gold eyes on bulges, folded hind legs, flat webbed feet. |
| frond_dragon | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Frond dragon: a leafy sea dragon about 40 cm long, a slender seahorse-like body held horizontal with a long tube snout and gold, olive, rose and amber leaf-like fronds all along it, so it looks like a drifting piece of kelp; drifts slowly among the kelp in twos or threes. |
| fruit_bat | base | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Fruit bat: a flying fox three times the size of a little bat, rust-collared and dog-faced, circling the fruiting trees in wide slow loops. |
| gallimimus | saurischia | saurischia | graze: Gallimimus: an ostrich-like dinosaur 5.5 m long and 1.9 m at the hip, with a small toothless beak on a long S-curved neck, long running legs, short arms and a long stiff tail held level. Sandy with a paler belly and a darker stripe down the back; runs in fast, flighty flocks over open grass. |
| gambels_quail | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | covey: Gambel's quail, a cock: plump and blue-grey, chestnut crown and flanks streaked white, a black face and throat edged white, a black patch on a buff belly, and a black teardrop plume curling forward off its head. Coveys of a dozen or more scuttle between the desert bushes in run-stop-peck, one now and then up on a bush top keeping watch. Pressed, the covey runs off on foot together, heads down; only when you are nearly on it does it burst up all at once on whirring wings, scatter low and come down a little way off. |
| gambels_quail_female | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Gambel's quail's female form (gambels_quail `forms`): the hen, plain grey-brown on the head with no black face or chestnut crown, a buff belly without the black patch, duller chestnut flanks, and a short brownish plume. Only its model is used. |
| gannet | gannet | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, vermilion | circle: circle with floats and plunge: Northern gannet: a big white seabird with a buff-yellow head, a dagger of a blue-grey bill and black wingtips, 1.8 m across. Circles twenty-odd metres over deep water on long stiff wings, a few quick beats and a long glide, then folds and drops straight in after fish with a splash, comes up a few seconds later, floats a while and climbs back to do it again. Built on the albatross's shape at 0.4 of its size. |
| garden_eel | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | school: Garden eels: a colony of thin spotted eels standing up out of their holes in the sand like a field of stalks, all facing into the current, and all sinking back into the sand as you swim near. v2: one tapered segment chain with a slight lean, a flattened head, eyes as small dark ellipsoids, spots by recolour. |
| garter_snake | glassfall | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, redrock, saurischia | Common garter snake, three quarters of a metre of olive and dark bands: the snake you actually meet, crossing a meadow path or lying out on a warm stone by the water. Quick to leave and carries no rattle. |
| giant_dragonfly | - | hemlow, saurischia | Giant dragonfly of the Grimwold: bronze-green, with a wingspan like a gull's, hunting along the Wend. |
| giant_millipede | - | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | Giant millipede: a metre of glossy black-brown segments on orange legs, slow over the moss and the deadfall in the Grimwold. |
| giant_octopus | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | creep: Giant Pacific octopus: brick red, mottled, three and a half metres across the arms. Lies on the sea floor with its arms let down over a rock or a fallen rib, creeps from one high spot to the next, and pales toward the colour of the sand as you swim near; pressed too hard, it jets off backwards with its arms streaming behind. Mantle, head and web are three overlapping ellipsoids; each arm is a chain of tapering segments with a curled tip. |
| giraffe | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | graze: Giraffe: five metres of leg and neck with a small body slung between them, browsing the tops of the thorn trees. Walks both legs of a side together and has no interest in people at all. Version 2 draws it for decimation: a sloping body of three overlapping ellipsoids, leg and neck as tapering segments, flattened hooves, ossicones two voxels thick, and brown patches painted by recolour. |
| giraffe_female | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The giraffe's female form (giraffe `forms`): a cow: shorter than the bull, with the hair still on her ossicones, dark tufts where his are worn bald. Only its model is used; it spawns as a giraffe. Written once from the giraffe's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| glass_dragonfly | faerie | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, lowmouth, saurischia | A dragonfly of the faerie wood with a glowing violet body and wings like thin glass. |
| glassmoth | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | flutter: A large moth with glowing wings. Comes in two coats: `violet`, seen near skystone, and `lantern`, warm gold and brighter, drifting low over lawns and under trees in the faerie wood. A biome picks one (`morph`). v3: lantern_moth merged in as the `lantern` coat, whose fourth number burns the wings a third brighter (a coat's glow gain); it is now the glassmoth's size, where the lantern moth was a fifth bigger. |
| glossy_starling | jadewater | jadewater, thornpan, thornpan_delta | flock: Glossy starling: a starling in metallic blue-green with a bright orange belly, in quick flocks round the towers and over the beaches. |
| glow_bee | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | A bee of the faerie wood: gold, softly glowing, drifting between blossoms at dusk. |
| goose | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | paddle: Canada goose, about 0.9 m long and 0.75 m to the top of the head. A boat-shaped body that sits low and level on water, a long black neck held up, a black head with a white chin strap, a flat broad bill, a brown back over paler flanks barred brown, white under a short black tail, black legs set back with webbed feet. Gaggles float near the shore, and lift off together, honking, when you come near. The base of every waterfowl: neck and head are one bone (`head`) drawn in the `neck`, `head` and `beak` colours so a child recolours them (mallard: green; swan: white); the back and flank bars use `wing`, the white strap and undertail `white`. Model drawn on a 66x48x52 grid at 0.025 m (30 voxels to the head), as few large overlapping shapes for decimation: a chest, body and rump ellipsoid, a tapering neck of three segments, a flattened bill, two-voxel eyes in their own `eye` colour (children's neck carve removes them; mallard and swan add their own), thick legs with flattened webbed feet. |
| gopher_snake | redrock | glassfall, jadewater, redrock, vermilion | climb: Great Basin gopher snake: cream with dark saddles, longer and slimmer than the rattlesnake it is often mistaken for, and no rattle. It climbs — up a pinyon or a ponderosa in a slow spiral, then out along a limb to lie with its head hanging off it — and comes down to cross open ground to the next tree. |
| great_egret | lowmouth | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | wade: Great egret: tall and all white with a yellow bill, stalking the shallows. |
| great_horned_owl | - | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | circle: Great horned owl: big, mottled grey-brown with a pale throat bib, tufted ears and a flat facial disc round big yellow eyes, quartering slowly over old forest at dusk. Rare: seen now and then, never for long. Base owl: barn and tawny owls inherit it and recolour `disc`, `ring`, `tufts`, `bib`, `mottle`, `body`, `wing`. Drawn at 0.0275 m voxels on a 56x32x32 grid. |
| green_tree_snake | jadewater | jadewater, redrock | climb: Slender green snake of the karst jungle: plain leaf-green above and yellow-cream below, so it is all but invisible until the hanging front of it sways. Spends its days draped along a limb or a bamboo cane and only crosses the ground to reach the next tree. |
| grouper | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | Grouper: a heavy brown fish nearly a metre long that hangs by a coral head or in a cave mouth and watches you. |
| gull | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia | circle: Herring gull, flying pose with wings spread; body, head, wings and tail built from a few large ellipsoids and segments. |
| harbor_seal | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | haulout: Harbour seal: grey with dark spots, hauled out on the rocks and sand in small groups. It watches you, then humps down to the sea and is gone with a splash. At sea it swims alone or in pairs among the kelp and along the shelf, turning to look at you. The base of every seal. v5: a plump spindle of two overlapping ellipsoids, a round head with a short muzzle and set-in eyes, flat flippers that lie against the flanks, and faster in the water. |
| hawk | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | soar: Red-tailed hawk: a stocky, broad-winged hawk, brown above with a pale buff belly crossed by a dark band, a rufous fan of tail and a short hooked bill, soaring in wide slow circles over open country and sitting on a high perch. Base hawk: eagle, sea eagle and peregrine inherit it and recolour `head`, `brow`, `cere`, `belly`, `band`, `tail`, `wing_tip`, `body`, `wing`. Drawn at 0.0225 m voxels on a 72x32x42 grid, in few large overlapping ellipsoids for the decimated meshes. |
| hedgehog | - | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, windbarrow | Hedgehog: a small brown ball of spines that trundles along the hedge bottoms at dusk and curls up tight when you come close. |
| heron | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | wade: Grey heron: stalks the shallows and stands still for long spells; when you come close it takes off on huge slow wings, flies thirty or forty metres along the water and lands again. The base of every long-legged wader. |
| herring | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow, zoo | school: Atlantic herring: a silver fish about 30 cm long with a blue-green back, in schools of ten to twenty near the shore. The base of every schooling fish. v3: deeper single-ellipsoid body with a pointed head, ellipsoid fins and a forked tail on a tapering peduncle, round eyes. |
| herring_shoal | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | Winter herring in the fjord: shoals of a hundred and more balled up tight a few metres under the surface, flashing silver as they turn, the reason the orcas and humpbacks come in. |
| hippo | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | wade: Hippo: three tonnes of grey barrel on stumps, eyes, ears and nostrils set on the top of the head so it can lie in a pool with everything else under the water. Gives way to nothing and runs faster than it looks. v3: two overlapping body ellipsoids, capsule legs, broad flattened muzzle. |
| horse | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | graze: Feral horse: bay with a black mane, tail and legs. Small bands graze open grass, a stallion keeping watch, and wheel away at a gallop. The base of every horse. |
| horse_juvenile | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The horse's juvenile form (horse `forms`): a foal, its legs nearly full length under a short, shallow body, so it stands tall for its size, with a brush of a mane and a short fluffy tail. Written once from the horse's ops; edit it as its own model. |
| humanoid | shooter | unused | skulk: The root of everything that stands on two legs and carries something in its hands: a plain human figure in neutral greys, named by what each colour is for (body, limb, face, foot, eye, dark), so a child is a different person by its colours and its own ops. Drawn for the smooth bake at 18.5 mm voxels, 95 tall on a 40x96x40 grid: the trunk as one loft of elliptical slices, a voxel row each, along a hip, waist, chest and shoulder profile (stacked ellipsoids pinched into balloons where they met), limbs as tapering segments held a voxel or two clear of the trunk so an arm swings without tearing the chest (the upper arms and thighs are loose bones, closed surfaces of their own, so the hip and shoulder show no hole when they swing), flattened-ellipsoid feet, mitten hands with a thumb, and a head with a jaw, nose, ears and eyes. Eighteen bones, three to a limb, driven by src/anim/figure.rs from a body's movement rather than by an activity table. Nothing lists it in a biome; the bot and zombie classes are built on it. |
| humpback | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | breach: Humpback whale, about 10 m long: black above with long white flippers, a pleated throat and a knobbly head. Alone offshore in deep water and rare: it dives for half a minute, lies at the surface spouting, and now and then breaches. |
| jack | underwater | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | Jacks: blue-silver fish in a fast school sweeping along the reef edge and round the bait balls. |
| jackrabbit | redrock | bonebight, gannet, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | Black-tailed jackrabbit: a big desert hare with long black-tipped ears that bolts early and runs far. |
| jaguar | base | jadewater | Jaguar: stockier and shorter-tailed than a cougar, golden under black rosettes, keeping to the river jungle. |
| javelina | redrock | redrock, vermilion | Collared peccary: a small grizzled grey-black pig of the desert with a pale band round the shoulders. Bands of four to ten root among the yucca and prickly ground, and huff off together when startled. |
| kangaroo_rat | base | redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, vermilion | Kangaroo rat: a pale sand-coloured desert rodent on long hind legs that comes out of its burrow after dark and bounds off in zigzags. |
| kelp_perch | underwater | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, kaldvik, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand | Kelp perch: small bronze fish in clouds round the kelp stipes, slipping in among them when startled. |
| killdeer | redrock | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saltmirror, vermilion | patter: Killdeer: a bigger, browner plover with two black bands across a white breast, an orange-red rump and longer legs. Run-stop-peck on bare ground, short grass, gravel bars and lake shores, alone or in twos and threes, a long way from any surf. |
| kingfisher | - | bonebight, gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | Kingfisher: a flash of electric blue with an orange breast, flitting between the willows and reeds along a river or round a cenote rim. v2: jewel_kingfisher merged in. |
| kite_ray | jadewater | jadewater | undulate: Kite ray: a jade-backed ray the size of a door, spotted with cyan light, that flies. Rises from the cenotes and rides the updrafts round the towers. |
| koi | bonebight | bonebight, jadewater | Koi in Kagami Pond, put there by whoever built the temple: carp half a metre long in red and white, gold, or plain white, cruising slowly near the surface in small groups. |
| langur | base | jadewater | Langur: pale silver-grey with a black face and hands, and a tail longer than it is, carried in a high loop. |
| lapwing | - | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, windbarrow | patter: Northern lapwing (peewit): a pigeon-sized plover of low wet pasture and short-grass meadow, black and white at a distance, with a long wispy crest swept up off the back of its head and a back of dark green that flashes green and purple when the sun catches it (its fourth colour value below zero is iridescence). Long pinkish legs; run, stop, tip forward to peck, in loose flocks out on the open turf; flushed, the flock goes up on broad round-ended wings with slow floppy beats and comes down again a field away. Remodelled v2 at 10 mm voxels: ellipsoid body, head with eyes and a swept segment crest, round-ended ellipsoid wings, segment legs with flat feet. |
| lechwe | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | wade: Red lechwe: a chestnut antelope of the flood meadows, hocks always wet, running in splashing herds through water a foot deep. The males carry long horns swept back in a shallow lyre. |
| lechwe_female | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The lechwe's female form (lechwe `forms`): a female: smaller than the male and hornless, as lechwe does are. Only its model is used; it spawns as a lechwe. Written once from the lechwe's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| lion | base | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Lion: a heavy tawny cat with a dark mane over its shoulders and a tufted tail. Lives in prides, so it walks in twos and threes. |
| lion_female | base | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Lioness: the lion's female form (lion `forms`), drawn when a lion is female. Smaller than the male and maneless, paler and sleeker, with a deep chest, a heavy square muzzle and the dark tuft on the tail. Only its model is used; it spawns as a lion. It hunts nothing of its own, so a world with lions doesn't build the cougar's prey for it. |
| lion_juvenile | base | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Lion cub: the lion's juvenile form (lion `forms`), drawn small (a juvenile's scale) with its big head and ears. Woollier and greyer-tawny than its mother, with faint brown spots on the flanks and legs that it loses as it grows, big rounded ears, and only a small dark tip to the tail. Only its model is used. |
| lions_mane_jelly | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Lion's mane jelly: a crimson-gold bell about 1.5 m across with a rim that glows faintly, frilled oral arms and a mass of long fine tentacles trailing 2 m or more below; drifts alone in mid-water, beating slowly. |
| lizard | redrock | peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | skulk: Side-blotched lizard: suns on warm rock and darts off when you come close. v2: segment legs and tail, flat feet, blotches by recolour. |
| lobster | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold | creep: American lobster, about half a metre with the claws: dark mottled olive-brown above, orange-tinged beneath, the claws and legs tipped red-orange, long red antennae. Lives on the rocky bay floor, lies up against a rock and walks slowly from one high spot to the next; pressed, it shoots off backwards with a flip of its tail. The creep mover pales it toward the sand as you come close, which a lobster would not do. v2: carapace, abdomen and tail fan are a few large overlapping ellipsoids; the abdomen and fan are their own `tail` bone so the body fits a 32^3 window; claws and legs are segments and flattened ellipsoids; eyes are small ellipsoids. |
| macaque | base | jadewater, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Macaque: grey-brown with a bare pink face and a stub of a tail, bold round temples and paths where people feed it. |
| mallard | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, vermilion, windbarrow | Mallard: grey-bodied drakes with green heads, a white neck ring and a chestnut breast among brown hens, dabbling in pairs and small groups. Short neck and head drawn over the goose's, in `head`, `neck` and `beak`. |
| mallard_female | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, vermilion, windbarrow | The mallard's female form (mallard `forms`): the hen, mottled brown all over with a darker crown, an orange-brown bill and no neck ring. Only its model is used; it spawns as a mallard. |
| mammoth | base | peregrine, windbarrow | graze: Woolly mammoth on its own 0.124 m voxel, a quarter finer than the elephant's scaled 1.38 and the same size in the world. A domed head with a topknot over a hump at the shoulder, a back that slopes to a low rump, small ears, a hairy trunk whose bare tip is its own bone, a skirt of dark fringe hanging below the belly (one flattened ellipsoid), tapered legs on flattened feet with round toenails, and long spiralling tusks built as a smooth tapering chain that sweep out, down and up with a stained base. Eyes are small dark ellipsoids set into the head. Its behaviour is the elephant's with the slower walk and wider groups of v2. |
| mammoth_juvenile | base | peregrine, windbarrow | The mammoth's juvenile form (mammoth `forms`): a calf with no tusks showing yet, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Written once from the mammoth's ops; edit it as its own model. |
| manta_ray | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow, zoo | school: Manta ray, about 4.5 m across: black above and white below, gliding alone or in pairs a little under the surface on slow ripples of its wings. Rare, and now and then it leaps clear. The base of every ray. Modelled as one smooth disc: a thick body with swept, pointed wings in three overlapping pieces a side, two head fins and a whip tail. |
| marbled_murrelet | - | gannet, hemlow, ribshore, ribstrand | circle: Marbled murrelet in breeding plumage on the razorbill's upright auk model: mottled dark brown all over with a paler brown belly and a short thin dark bill. Nests high on the limbs of the old redwoods at Loftwood and flies down to the bay at dawn. |
| marmot | glassfall | glassfall, peregrine, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | graze: Lives in the scree: a long, heavy-bodied ground squirrel, grizzled brown with a pale muzzle and a bushy dark-tipped tail. Sits up on a boulder to whistle. v2: two overlapping body ellipsoids, capsule legs with flattened paws, ellipsoid head, muzzle, ears and eyes. Drawn at 15 voxels per height, not the rule's 20 (Rob's choice, 2026-11-23): its long body made 20 cost more than a deer. |
| marsh_light | - | glassfall, hemlow, lowmouth, saurischia, windbarrow | drift: A will-o'-the-wisp of the Wisp Moss: a pale blue-white ball of light the size of a fist with a fainter halo, drifting low over the bog pools and slowly pulsing. Nobody knows what it is. Comes in two lights: the cold `wisp` of the Wisp Moss, and the yellow `corpse` candle that drifts between the barrows after dark, which the villages say is the breath going by. A biome picks one (`morph`). v3: corpse_candle merged in as the `corpse` coat; it moves as the candle did (`drift`, a wider and lazier circle than `flutter`), and the halo's blue came down a little so one tint reaches the candle's yellow. |
| meadowhawk | redrock | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | Red meadowhawk: a smaller dragonfly, bright red, perching low over the canyon's backwaters and the lake shore. |
| mola | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | school: Ocean sunfish: a tall flat grey disc nearly three metres from fin tip to fin tip, with no tail to speak of but a rounded clavus at the back. Hangs alone just under the surface over deep water, its dorsal fin now and then cutting the swell, and lets you swim right up to it. |
| monkey | base | jadewater, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | skulk: A brown monkey of the river jungle: goes on all fours with its tail up behind it, in troops that keep together and scatter uphill when you come. Modelled v2 for decimation: capsule limbs and tail, flattened hands and feet, a muzzle and set-in eyes. |
| moon_jelly | underwater | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | school: Moon jellyfish: a pale lilac bell the size of a plate with four rings in it, drifting, beating slowly upward and sinking between. v2: one broad domed bell, four oral arms and a skirt of rim tentacles drawn as segments. |
| morpho | kitehold | kitehold | flutter: Blue morpho: a butterfly 15 cm across the wings, brilliant metallic blue above with black borders and brown below with two dark spots on each wing. Wings are two-voxel plates (brown below, blue above); the body has eyes and club-tipped antennae. It drifts in slow flutters low under the cloud forest canopy and along rides in the light. |
| moss_bear | faerie | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's bear: larger and slower than a black bear, its coat gone green-brown under a mantle of moss, with pale mushrooms on its back that glow at dusk. It barely minds people. |
| moss_tortoise | faerie | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The faerie wood's tortoise: big and ancient, its shell grown over with moss and small pale mushrooms that glow at dusk. |
| mossback_deer | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | A deer with moss grown along its back and antlers that glow faintly green. Shyer than a red deer. |
| mossback_deer_female | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | The mossback deer's female form (mossback_deer `forms`): a doe: smaller than the buck and without antlers. Only its model is used; it spawns as a mossback deer. Written once from the mossback deer's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
