| mule_deer | redrock | redrock, vermilion | Mule deer: grey-brown desert deer with big ears and a black-tipped tail. |
| mule_deer_female | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The mule deer's female form (mule_deer `forms`): a doe: smaller than the buck and without antlers. Only its model is used; it spawns as a mule deer. Written once from the mule deer's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| mule_deer_juvenile | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The mule deer's juvenile form (mule_deer `forms`): a spotted fawn, the hornless female's body with white spots in rows down the back and flanks, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| nightjar | base | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | circle: Nightjar: a bark-mottled bird with a wide gape that sits unseen on the ground by day and hawks moths low over heath, down and dune at dusk. |
| northern_flicker | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, redrock, vermilion | Northern flicker: a big brown woodpecker barred with black, spotted below, with a red moustache. Often feeds on the ground and in open woods. |
| nutria | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | wade: Nutria: a big brown water rat with orange teeth, grazing the banks and swimming off when you come. |
| old_pale | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Old Pale: a white alligator half as long again as any other, seen now and then in the swamp and never twice in the same place. |
| opal_squid | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Opal squid: a small squid about 35 cm long with big eyes and an iridescent pearl-white body patched with rose, violet and teal; hangs in the water in shoals of 15 to 30. |
| orca | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | breach: Orcas: a family of four or five following the herring into the fjord in winter, black and white, the bull's fin a head taller than the rest. They breach less than the humpbacks and travel faster. |
| otter | base | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | wade: River otter: a long low swimmer with a thick tapering tail, up on the bank to eat and back in the water as soon as it sees you. |
| pale_hare | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | A hare bleached like the trees in the bloom. It does not run from people. |
| panda | jadewater | jadewater | graze: Giant panda: white with black legs, shoulders, ears and eye patches. Sits in the bamboo eating; slow, and not much bothered by people. |
| parasaurolophus | dinosaurs | saurischia | graze: Parasaurolophus: a duck-billed hadrosaur, 9.5 m long, with a hollow crest sweeping back a metre and a half from the top of its head. Grazes on all fours with the back sloping up to tall hips, and runs from danger. Olive-green with dark bars down the back and flanks, pale beneath, the crest rust-red. Lives in herds of a dozen and more that keep close together. Drawn at 105 mm voxels: the neck, crest and tail are tapering segment chains, the bill and feet flattened ellipsoids. |
| parrotfish | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | Parrotfish: half a metre long, teal and pink, crunching coral with its beak; the white sand of the reef is what it leaves. |
| peregrine | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, saurischia | circle: Peregrine falcon: slate-blue back, barred pale breast, a dark hood and moustache and long pointed wings. A pair nest on Raven Scar and circle high over the vale. |
| pileated_woodpecker | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Pileated woodpecker: crow-sized, black with a flaming red crest and a white stripe down the neck. Hacks big oblong holes in snags and old trunks. |
| poison_frog | kitehold | kitehold | hop: Poison dart frog: a thumb-sized frog of the cloud forest floor, bright blue with black spots, hopping about in the leaf litter by day. Its legs have their own colour, `limb`, so a rare child can be red with blue legs. |
| pond_turtle | saurischia | saurischia | bask: Pond turtle, a shell about 30 cm long: dark olive-brown, low and smooth, the skin streaked yellow. Basks on the banks of slow streams and slides into the water when you come close. |
| prairie_dog | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | burrow: Black-tailed prairie dog: a plump, sandy ground squirrel with a pale belly and a short dark-tipped tail, in colonies on short grass; sits up on its haunches to look round. |
| pronghorn | redrock | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | graze: Pronghorn: tan and white, short black horns with a forward prong, the fastest thing on the open flats. Modelled for decimation on 41.7 mm voxels: deep-chested barrel, slim segment legs with knees, dark hooves, white belly, rump, throat bands and cheeks, dark nose and eyes. Small herds. |
| pronghorn_female | redrock | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The pronghorn's female form (pronghorn `forms`): a doe: a little smaller than the buck, with short horns about the length of her ears and no prong. Only its model is used; it spawns as a pronghorn. Written once from the pronghorn's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| pronghorn_juvenile | redrock | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | The pronghorn's juvenile form (pronghorn `forms`): a fawn, the female's body without her horns, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| protoceratops | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | graze: Protoceratops: a sheep-sized frilled herbivore about 1.8 m long, the hornless little cousin of triceratops, beaked and stocky, sandy olive with a darker frill. Grazes in herds in the open woods and glades, and is what the velociraptors hunt. |
| przewalski_horse | windbarrow | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock, peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | graze: Przewalski's horse: dun, pale round the muzzle and belly, with a short upright black mane and a dark stripe down the back. Bands of mares graze the open grass and wheel away together. |
| ptarmigan | kaldvik | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | flock: Rock ptarmigan in winter white, crouched in the snow on the fell in small coveys and only seen when they burst up from under your feet. They fly fast and low on whirring wings when flushed. |
| pteranodon | dinosaurs | hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | glide: Pteranodon: a pterosaur 6.5 m across the wings, a small white body slung between long leathery wings, a long toothless beak and a crest sweeping back from the head. Soars over the sea on held wings in small parties, like the albatross, and comes down together on high bare ground, where it stands on its forelimbs with each wing finger folded up and back from the hand, the tips high over its back. |
| puffin | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, ribshore | circle: Atlantic puffin on the razorbill's upright auk model: black back and collar, white face and belly, a tall triangular bill banded grey-blue, yellow and orange, orange feet. Circles low off the basalt cliffs where it nests and rafts on the sea. |
| quetzal | kitehold | kitehold | flit: Resplendent quetzal: a pigeon-sized bird of the cloud forest, iridescent emerald above with a crimson belly, a rounded crest and two green tail streamers about twice its body length. It sits in the canopy and flits from top to top. |
| quetzalcoatlus | saurischia | saurischia | graze: Quetzalcoatlus: a giant pterosaur about 5 m tall at the head, stalking the grass on all fours. It stands on its forelimbs with each wing finger folded up and back from the hand and the dark membrane bunched between finger and arm; startled, it vaults into the air and flies off on wings about 12 m across. Pale grey with darker wing membranes, a long stiff neck, a huge tapering beak and a reddish crest; slow, solitary and wary of people. |
| rabbit | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | hop: Brown rabbit: long ears with pink insides, white tail, pale belly, long hind feet. |
| raccoon | base | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Raccoon: a grizzled grey forager with a black mask and a ringed tail, out after dark along creek banks, levees and the backs of towns, slow to bolt and quick to come back. |
| rattlesnake | redrock | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | slither: Western rattlesnake, about 1.2 m: tan with dark bands and a pale belly, a diamond head wider than its neck with gold eyes, and a pale rattle on the tail. Lies coiled or stretched out on warm ground, crawls with its body sliding through a wave that stays where it is put, and when you come close it winds up tight, lifts its head at you and buzzes rather than leaving. The base of every snake in the library. |
| raven | kaldvik | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | circle: Ravens: a pair that tumble and croak over the hamlet and the stockfish racks all winter. |
| razorbill | gannet | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, ribshore, ribstrand | circle: Razorbill, the base of the diving auks. Stands upright on land like a small penguin, legs far back, and sits low on the sea; black above and sharply white below, a deep blade-shaped black bill crossed by a white line (`bill_stripe`) and a fine white line from the bill to the eye (`face_line`). Short narrow wings with a white trailing edge, a short pointed tail. Nests on cliff ledges beside the puffins, circles low off the cliffs and dives for fish. Puffin, murrelet and the like inherit it: recolour `bill`, `bill_stripe`, `belly`, `face_line` or swap the bill ops. |
| red_squirrel | base | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, saurischia | Red squirrel: smaller than the grey, rust-red with a cream front and tufted ears, keeping to the conifers and the old hedgerow trees. |
| reef_chromis | underwater | gannet, glassfall, jadewater | Blue-green chromis: finger-length fish in a shimmering cloud over one coral head, dropping into it all at once when something comes too near. |
| reef_manta | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The manta at home on the reef: gliding along the wall and over the fore reef in ones and threes, coming to the cleaning stations on the spurs. Common here where the open-sea manta is rare, and as unafraid. |
| reef_shark | underwater | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Grey reef shark: about 1.6 m, grey above and white below, cruising the wall and the pass in long slow passes. It minds nobody. |
| roadrunner | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, vermilion | dash: Greater roadrunner: a long-legged, long-tailed cuckoo of the desert, streaked brown and buff, with a shaggy dark crest and a patch of blue and red bare skin behind the eye. Alone, it trots and sprints across open ground, stops with its tail cocked, hops up onto a rock or a bush to look round, runs down lizards and carries them off in its bill. Startled, it mostly runs; now and then it flaps up and glides low a little way. |
| rock_hyrax | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | skulk: Rock hyrax: a stout grey-brown animal the size of a rabbit with no tail to speak of, in colonies on the quartzite crags, basking in a row on the warm rock in the morning. The eagles hunt them. |
| rockfish | underwater | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | Rockfish: orange-red and brown, loose schools hanging in the middle water between the kelp stipes. |
| roseate_spoonbill | lowmouth | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | wade: Roseate spoonbill: a pink wader sweeping its long spoon-ended bill through the shallows of the mouth and the flats. v2: capsule legs and neck, flattened spoon bill, ellipsoid wings. |
| sabretooth | windbarrow | peregrine, windbarrow | skulk: Sabretoothed cat: heavier in the shoulder than a cougar and tawny with faint flank spots, a pair of long pale canines hanging past its jaw. Hunts horses and saiga, alone, and is rarely seen. |
| saiga | windbarrow | peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | graze: Saiga: a pale sandy steppe antelope with a swollen down-turned nose and short ridged amber horns. Small groups out on the open grass; the quickest thing here to run. |
| saiga_female | windbarrow | peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | The saiga's female form (saiga `forms`): a female: smaller than the male and hornless, as saiga females are. Only its model is used; it spawns as a saiga. Written once from the saiga's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| salmon | gannet | bonebight, gannet, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, ribshore, ribstrand | Salmon, about 75 cm: steel-blue and silver fresh from the sea, going dark bronze and coppery red as it runs up the rivers in autumn to spawn. Holds in small groups in the deep pools and leaps often. |
| salt_tortoise | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | plod: The salt tortoise: twice the height of an elephant (a shell 7.4 m long and 4.8 m high), its high domed shell bleached grey-white, plated with scutes whose growth rings show, the rim worn and nicked, and crusted with salt on the back and low down where it lies in the brine. A heavy wrinkled head on a thick neck, pillar legs with big scales on the front. A few of them cross the Mirror, very slowly, from the brine to the islands and back, and take no notice of anyone. When you come close it draws head, legs and tail in under the rim. Nobody knows how old they are. |
| sanderling | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, ribshore, ribstrand | scurry: Sanderling in winter grey: a small pale sandpiper that keeps to the very edge of the surf in flocks, running up the beach ahead of each wave on legs gone to a blur and chasing the water back down to probe the wet sand it leaves. Flushed, the flock swings out low over the breakers, white wing bars flashing, and comes in again farther along. Drawn on 6 mm voxels, 20 along its height, as a few overlapping ellipsoids and segments so it bakes smooth; the base of the small surf-runners. |
| sandhill_crane | redrock | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | Sandhill crane: tall and grey with a red crown and a bustle of feathers over the tail. Pairs and small groups stalk wet meadows and river flats, calling, and lift off on broad slow wings. |
| sardine | underwater | glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, megalith, peregrine, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | Sardines: a bait ball of a hundred or more, turning together, opening round a diver and closing behind. v2: the herring shoal's bait ball, smaller and bluer and a little deeper, rather than a second copy of it. |
| sea_eagle | kaldvik | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | soar: A white-tailed sea eagle: broad brown wings like a barn door, a pale head and the white wedge of tail, quartering the fjord for fish. |
| sea_lion | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, ribshore, ribstrand | Steller sea lion: big and tawny, louder and bolder than the seals, hauled out in groups on the basalt. Long in the neck, with big wing-like foreflippers it flies through the water on, fast and turning tight. v3: three overlapping body ellipsoids, a segment neck, paddle foreflippers set out from the shoulder. |
| sea_lion_male | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, ribshore, ribstrand | The sea lion's male form (sea_lion `forms`): the bull, half again the cow's weight, with a thick maned neck and chest and a raised crest on the crown. Only its model is used; it spawns as a sea lion. |
| sea_otter | underwater | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Sea otter: lying on its back in the kelp canopy, drifting, rolling now and then; it pays a diver little mind. |
| sea_turtle | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, megalith, peregrine, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, windbarrow, zoo | bask: Green sea turtle, a metre long: a rounded olive shell and long front flippers that join the shell's side under the rim. Hauls out on the sand beaches to bask and drags itself along; when you come close it pulls its head and flippers in under the shell. Now and then it crawls back to the sea and swims off. |
| serow | jadewater | jadewater | graze: Serow: a small dark goat-antelope of the tower ledges and the top of the Old Wall, with a pale throat. |
| serow_female | jadewater | jadewater | The serow's female form (serow `forms`): a female: much like the male, with horns a little thinner. Only its model is used; it spawns as a serow. Written once from the serow's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| serow_juvenile | jadewater | jadewater | The serow's juvenile form (serow `forms`): a kid, the female's body without her horns, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| sheep | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, peregrine, redrock, windbarrow | graze: Sheep: the bighorn with its horns taken off and a fleece grown over it, white with a dark face and legs, out on the pasture in flocks. |
| sheep_female | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, peregrine, redrock, windbarrow | The sheep's female form (sheep `forms`): a ewe: smaller than the ram and polled, as most domestic ewes are. Only its model is used; it spawns as a sheep. Written once from the sheep's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| sixgill_shark | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Bluntnose sixgill shark: four and a half metres of dark brown-grey with one low dorsal fin set far back, six gill slits and pale green eyes. Cruises slowly a metre or two over the deep sea floor, minding nobody; rare. |
| skunk | base | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saltmirror, vermilion | Striped skunk: black with two white stripes from crown to a big plumed tail, ambling through field edges and open woods at night and hardly bothering to get out of your way. |
| sky_manta | windbarrow | peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | undulate: One skate in twenty grown to three metres across, riding well above the rest where they skim the awns. A rare morph of wind_skate, so it stands in for one of them and is never spawned on its own. |
| sky_ray | faerie | gannet, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, peregrine, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | undulate: A ray that swims through the air of the faerie wood, a metre and a half across, violet-backed with glowing spots, circling slowly a few metres up on rippling wings. |
| skylark | - | bonebight, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, kitehold_rock, saurischia | Skylark: a small streaked brown bird of the open down and the heath, rising to sing high overhead. |
| snowy_plover | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | patter: Snowy plover: a round little sand-coloured plover with a big head and a stub of a bill, almost invisible on dry sand until it moves. Runs a few metres on twinkling legs, stops dead, tips forward to peck, runs again; scattered loose flocks on the upper beach, the wrack line, dunes and salt flats, out of the surf's way. The base of the plovers (killdeer is like it). |
| sparrow | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | flit: A small brown sparrow that sits on top of shrubs and small trees, flits from one to the next in short bounding hops, drops to the ground to peck, and bursts away when you come close. Groups of two to six. |
| spawning_salmon | bonebight | bonebight, kitehold | Salmon on the spawning run up the delta's clear channel: crimson flanks and olive-green heads, holding in knee-deep water over the cobbles. |
| spectacled_bear | kitehold | kitehold | Spectacled bear: the shaggy, black Andean bear with a cream muzzle and cream arcs round the eyes running down the throat and chest; lives in cloud forest and paramo. |
| spotted_hyena | base | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Spotted hyena: a sloping sandy-grey dog-like hunter with dark spots, travelling the savanna and the pan edges in loose clans after dark. |
| squirrel | base | bonebight, ctfbox, duelbox, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saurischia, slagdeck, sluice, vermilion | hop: Grey squirrel: bounds over the ground between trees with its tail plumed up behind it, and flicks away round a trunk when you come. Remodelled at 16 mm voxels as a hunched body with a haunch, segment limbs and tail, and a snout and eyes. |
| starling | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | flock: Starlings: big flocks that wheel over open ground, settle in a meadow or a tree, and burst up together when you come near. Also the body every perching bird in the library starts from (crow, eagle, sparrow are `like` it). |
| stegosaurus | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | graze: Stegosaurus: a 8.5 m plated dinosaur with a small low head, a back arched high over the hips and short forelegs on long hind legs. Two staggered rows of tall reddish plates, drawn as flattened ellipsoids two voxels thick, run along the back, and four tapering spikes sit near the tail tip. Grazes the forest edges in small groups and keeps its distance. |
| stellers_jay | - | bonebight, gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | Steller's jay: a loud crested jay of the redwoods, charcoal head and shining blue body. |
| steppe_sheep | windbarrow | peregrine, windbarrow | graze: The sheep of the villages, kept in one big flock apiece rather than in knots of a few: twelve to thirty-six together, so a flock is a sight when you come on it and the downs are empty between. |
| steppe_sheep_female | windbarrow | peregrine, windbarrow | The steppe sheep's female form (steppe_sheep `forms`): a ewe: smaller than the ram and polled. Only its model is used; it spawns as a steppe sheep. Written once from the steppe sheep's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| stingray | underwater | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, lowmouth, ribshore, ribstrand | Southern stingray: a sandy diamond a metre and a bit across, lying half buried on the sand of the grooves and the lagoon, gliding off when disturbed. |
| swan | glassfall | bonebight, glassfall, hemlow, lowmouth, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | Trumpeter swan: big and white with a black bill, alone or in pairs on quiet water, and slow to leave it. The goose's body with the neck redrawn about twice as long, in an S. |
| swordbill | kitehold | kitehold | hum: Sword-billed hummingbird of the high Andes: a dark bronze-green hummingbird whose bill is longer than its body. It sits on a bare twig and works the long trumpet flowers of the cloud forest edge. The Anna's hummingbird model redrawn with the bill and wings lengthened, since the bill needs more grid in front. v2: two overlapping body ellipsoids, a tapering segment bill on its own bone, flattened-ellipsoid wings, tail and feet for the decimated mesh. |
| tawny_owl | - | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, saurischia, windbarrow | circle: Tawny owl: a round, rufous-brown wood owl with a streaked front, a round pale-brown face with a darker rim and dark eyes, floating low between the trunks of the Hollins at dusk. Built on great_horned_owl, tufts removed. |
| tiger | base | jadewater | Tiger: the cougar's frame half again as big, orange over a white belly and barred head to tail, walking the bamboo alone. |
| toad | base | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, vermilion, windbarrow | Common toad: squatter and warty, dry brown, walking more than hopping and found well away from water as often as beside it. |
| tortoise | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, megalith, peregrine, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow, zoo | plod: A tortoise about 45 cm long: a high domed brown carapace on one large ellipsoid, plated with vertebral and costal scutes and a flared rim, a rounded head with dark eyes on a short neck, thick columnar front legs with dark scaly fronts and flat feet, stumpy hind legs and a stub of a tail. It plods, crops low plants, and when you come close pulls its head, legs and tail in under the shell until you go. The base of every tortoise and turtle. |
| triceratops | dinosaurs | kitehold, saurischia | graze: Triceratops: the three-horned dinosaur, 9 m long and 3 m at the hips, heavy-bodied on four strong legs, with a great bony frill behind the head, two long brow horns, a short nose horn and a parrot beak. Olive-grey and mottled; the frill is rust with a dark knobbed rim and a pale spot each side. Grazes low in small herds. Drawn at 200 mm voxels as two overlapping body ellipsoids, capsule legs, neck and tail, a three-slab frill and capsule horns. |
| triceratops_juvenile | dinosaurs | kitehold, saurischia | The triceratops' juvenile form (triceratops `forms`): its brow horns short stubs and its nose horn a knob, as in young triceratops. No female form, since no difference between the sexes is known. Only its model is used. |
| trout | redrock | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | Rainbow trout stocked in the Redrock reservoir: olive back, pale sides, in loose groups of three to six, and quicker to jump than herring. |
| tyrannosaurus | dinosaurs | saurischia | skulk: Tyrannosaurus: the great tyrant, 12 m long and over 3 m at the hips, with a deep, broad skull full of teeth, tiny two-fingered arms and a tail held out straight behind. Dark olive-brown above with faint darker bars, tan beneath. Walks alone, slowly, and hunts the hadrosaurs and horned dinosaurs; it fears nothing. Modelled in two body ellipsoids, capsule chains for neck, tail and legs, and a skull of overlapping ellipsoids. |
| unicorn | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, kitehold_rock, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion, windbarrow | One deer in a hundred is born white with a single faintly glowing horn and no antlers. It lives and runs with ordinary deer and is harder to get near. |
| utahraptor | dinosaurs | kitehold, saurischia | skulk: Utahraptor: the great raptor, 6 m long and 1.5 m at the hips, heavily built and feathered all over, with feathered arms like short wings, a fan of feathers at the end of a stiff tail and the curved sickle claw held up off the ground on each foot. Chestnut with dark bars, cream beneath, the wing and tail feathers tipped pale. Hunts in pairs and threes. |
| velociraptor | dinosaurs | kitehold, saurischia | skulk: Velociraptor: a small raptor the size of a big turkey, 2 m long and half a metre at the hips, slim and long-legged, with a long low snout, feathered arms and tail fan and the sickle claw on each foot. Sandy buff spotted with brown, the wing feathers dark with pale tips. Hunts small animals in twos and threes. v2 at 28 mm voxels: capsule limbs, neck and tail, flat feathered arm blades and tail fan, eyes as ellipsoids. |
| vicuna | kitehold | kitehold | graze: Vicuna: the wild Andean camelid, cinnamon-fawn with a white belly, chest bib and inner legs, a long slender neck carrying a small head high. Grazes in family groups on high moor. v2: remodelled for decimation at 48.3 mm voxels. |
| vulture | thornpan | saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta | soar: White-backed vulture: a brown soaring plank with a bald head, circling anything dead. Where there are three in the air there is a kill under them. |
| walrus | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | haulout: Pacific walrus: three metres of wrinkled cinnamon hide with pale tusks and a bristled muzzle. Hauls out in heaps on the beach and the low rocks, barely lifts its head when you walk past, and lumbers into the surf only when you all but step on it. v2: propped up on its foreflippers with its chest and tusked head high, and lying level in the water. |
| warthog | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Warthog: grey, bristled and comic, head down in the grass with its tail straight up when it trots off. Everywhere in the country and the first thing most people see. |
| water_buffalo | base | jadewater, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Water buffalo: slate grey, low-slung and wide, under a great pair of horns swept back in a crescent. Keeps to the wet ground and wallows. |
| water_buffalo_female | base | jadewater, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | The water buffalo's female form (water_buffalo `forms`): a cow: smaller than the bull, with lighter horns. Only its model is used; it spawns as a water buffalo. Written once from the water buffalo's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| water_buffalo_juvenile | base | jadewater, redrock, thornpan, thornpan_delta | The water buffalo's juvenile form (water_buffalo `forms`): a calf, the female's body without her horns, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| water_snake | lowmouth | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | swim: Dark banded water snake of the swamp and the sawgrass: swims at the surface with its head out and a long wave running down its body, ducks under when you press it, and hauls out on a bank or a cypress knee to lie in the sun. |
| whale_shark | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | school: Whale shark: eight metres of dark blue-grey spotted with white, wide flat head with a terminal mouth, tall first dorsal fin, broad pectorals, a crescent tail. Harmless and unafraid; hold on and it takes you with it. Rare. v2 redrawn for decimation: few large overlapping body ellipsoids, flattened-ellipsoid fins, spots by recolour. |
| white_lizard | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | skulk: Bleached earless lizard: the dunes' own lizard, gone nearly as white as the gypsum it runs on, with faint blue on its flanks. |
| wildebeest | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Blue wildebeest: slate grey, high in the shoulder and low in the head, with a black mane and beard and horns that hook out and up. Grazes the dry grass in long strung-out herds. |
| wildebeest_female | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The wildebeest's female form (wildebeest `forms`): a cow: a little smaller than the bull, with thinner horns and a slighter boss. Only its model is used; it spawns as a wildebeest. Written once from the wildebeest's own ops; edit it as its own model. |
| wildebeest_juvenile | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The wildebeest's juvenile form (wildebeest `forms`): a calf, the female's body without her horns, drawn small at a juvenile's scale with its big head. Only its model is used. |
| wind_skate | windbarrow | peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | undulate: Wind skate: a straw-pale ray a metre across that rides the wind over the feathergrass, a hand above the awns, wings rippling. Nobody knows what it eats. |
| wolf | base | bonebight, glassfall, hemlow, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, peregrine, redrock, saurischia, windbarrow | Grey wolf: a quarter bigger than a coyote and heavier in the shoulder, hunting in packs of two to six: deer and elk in the forests, horses and saiga on the steppe. Keeps its distance and watches. v2: steppe_wolf merged in (its prey and its larger packs). |
| wolf_eel | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | school: Wolf eel: about 2 m long, a grey-olive tapering body with dark spots, a big blunt head with heavy jaws and one dorsal fin the whole way back; holds near the rocky bottom and moves slowly. |
| woodpecker | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, saurischia, thornpan, thornpan_delta, vermilion | cling: Downy woodpecker: small, black and white with a red patch on the head. Clings upright to trunks, drums, hitches up and round them, and bounds off to the next tree. The base of every woodpecker. |
| yellow_tang | underwater | gannet, glassfall, jadewater | Yellow tang: all lemon-yellow, grazing over the coral in small loose groups. |
| zebra | glassfall | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Plains zebra: a striped horse. A small herd brought to the island for a menagerie that never opened, and they stayed. |
| zebra_juvenile | glassfall | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The zebra's juvenile form (zebra `forms`): a foal, long-legged under a short body like the horse's, its stripes brown on cream rather than black on white. Written once from the zebra's ops; edit it as its own model. |
| zombie | shooter | unused | skulk: The zombie class root: a dead, nearly human figure built on the humanoid at the same voxel size, in torn grey clothes. The head is drawn with care for the smooth bake: brow ridge, sunken sockets with dull pale eyes, cheek hollows, a nose, ears, and lips drawn back over teeth, with the lower jaw and teeth on the jaw bone so the mouth can hang open. Dead grey-green skin with bruising and a few wound patches painted by recolour. It stands upright with arms clear of the trunk; the shamble and the hunch come from animation. |
| zombie_hiker | shooter | unused | A dead hiker: a hooded rain jacket with the hood down behind the head, a pack on the back, walking trousers torn open at the left shin, heavy boots. Bulky padded outline. |
| zombie_labourer | shooter | unused | A dead labourer: big and wide-shouldered, denim overalls with bib and straps over a rust work shirt rolled to bare forearms, heavy hands, a yellow hard hat on the head bone. Heavier limbs and trunk than the humanoid, with its joints moved out to match. |
| zombie_office | shooter | unused | A dead office worker: a dingy shirt untucked over slacks, a loose red tie, the right sleeve torn back from the forearm, the right shoe gone so a sock shows, dark hair. |

## Collectibles

| name | from | used by | notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| acorn | displays | unused | Not found lying about: what jays, crows, ravens and acorn woodpeckers carry when they cache (a display's food). |
| antler_pick | windbarrow | windbarrow | A red-deer antler with its tines cut back to one, worn smooth at the grip: the tool the flint comes out with. |
| arrowhead | redrock | gannet, kaldvik, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, vermilion | A chipped obsidian point from the canyon people, on the benches and the canyon floor. |
| bear_tooth | windbarrow | kitehold, windbarrow | A cave bear's canine, as long as a finger, with a hole bored through the root: picked up round the cave mouths and worn. |
| bell_charm | jadewater | jadewater, kitehold | A little bronze bell on a red cord, left at the temple by the Order's visitors. |
| flint_blade | windbarrow | kitehold, peregrine, thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | A long blade struck from a flint core, blue-black with a white heel: on the working floors and all along the trails. |
| hag_stone | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | A pebble with a hole worn right through it. The village says if you look through one you see the Hollins as they really are. |
| hull_shard | saltmirror | saltmirror | A piece of the hull's skin lying in the fynbos below the wreck: dark, very light for its size, with a violet sheen and an edge that is not quite cold. |
| ivory_shard | windbarrow | thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | A curved splinter off a mammoth tusk, weathered out of the chalk: cream going brown along the cracks. |
| mushroom | glassfall | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, kitehold, saurischia | Fly agaric, in the woods. |
| petrified_wood | redrock | redrock | A chunk of log turned to stone, from the banded clay badlands. |
| pine_cone | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, redrock, vermilion | Under the conifers. |
| pottery_shard | glassfall | glassfall, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, vermilion, windbarrow | Pieces of the settlers’ crockery, around the village. |
| purple_bottle | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, jadewater, kaldvik, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, redrock, ribshore, saltmirror, thornpan, thornpan_delta | An old clear bottle turned violet by a century of desert sun, around Harlow. |
| seashell | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array, thornpan, thornpan_delta | Washed up on the beaches. |
| settler_coin | glassfall | glassfall, kitehold, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | A brass coin with a hole in the middle; it glints in the grass. |
| silver_ore | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, redrock, saltmirror | A lump of galena-bearing ore that fell off a Harlow ore cart. |
| skystone | glassfall | glassfall | A buried fragment from the strewn field: black glass with violet glints, faintly warm. |
| skystone_chip | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | A splinter of skystone lying in the bloom. |
| skystone_mother | glassfall | glassfall | The main mass of the thing that fell, buried deep at the far end of the strewn field. |

## Biomes

| name | from | used by | notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| arch_country | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The slickrock bench east of the canyon where the fins stand: bare sandstone and red sand worn into arches, windows and potholes. Few plants. |
| arena_seabed | slagdeck | ctfbox, duelbox, porch, slagdeck, sluice, transmission | The sea floor round an arena: sand and nothing living on it, so no whale or shoal turns up under a deck. The open-world seabed with its animals taken out. |
| aspen_draw | vermilion | vermilion | A damp ravine cut into a mesa's face by a spring-fed stream: a grove of aspen on grass along the water, a few young ponderosa and gambel oak on the drier banks, willow and thimbleberry by the stream. |
| backwater_flats | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | The delta's quiet water: eelgrass over silt in the sheltered reaches behind the islands, brown and barely moving, with rafts of lily pads on the surface and hyacinth where it is stillest. The fresh-water twin of eelgrass_flats, which is the salt meadow of a lagoon. |
| bamboo_forest | jadewater | jadewater | Wetter ground east of the Old Wall: giant bamboo in dense patches over long green grass, among small broadleaf trees and ferns, misty. |
| bamboo_gorge | jadewater | jadewater | The narrow misty gorges of the Old Wall: golden bamboo you push through, a few giant clumps, ferns, pandas. |
| barley_plot | windbarrow | kitehold, windbarrow | A barley plot behind its hurdles: thin ranks of pale ears on worked chalk soil, a few thistles at the fence line. |
| barrow_field | windbarrow | windbarrow | The barrow ridge: turf kept short by the herds over nineteen mounds, chalk kerbs showing through it, oak posts on the crowns, and nothing growing taller than a thistle. |
| basalt_coast | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik |  |
| basalt_crater | redrock | redrock, saurischia | The floor and inner walls of a young cone's crater: black basalt the last lava froze into, broken columns, fallen blocks, and the mouths of the tubes it drained through. Nothing grows yet. |
| bb_dunes | bonebight | bonebight | Bonebight's dunes: Ribshore's dune grass and buckthorn, with teeth and loose vertebrae of the broken-up titan lying where the storms dropped them. |
| bb_marsh | bonebight | bonebight | The Lessa's marsh on the north shore of the bay: reed and cattail on hummocks between tidal channels. |
| bb_plain | bonebight | bonebight | The last of the Kitehold Plain, sloping down off the ash and loess into the dunes: the same gold bunchgrass, thinning toward the sand. |
| bb_salmon_run | bonebight | bonebight | The clear channel of the delta where the salmon run: knee-deep water over dark cobbles, flat gravel banks with a few willows, where the black bears come down to fish. |
| bb_spruce_hollow | bonebight | bonebight | The wet hollows of the spruce wood along Mirror Creek: alder and red osier among the spruce, ferns knee-deep, moss on everything. |
| bb_temple | bonebight | bonebight | The wood round Kagami Pond: coast redwoods and old spruce over moss and redwood sorrel, Japanese maples along the shore that go crimson in autumn, ferns, and stone grown over with moss. Koi in the pond. |
| bb_town | bonebight | bonebight | The lanes and yards of Bonebight inside the ribcage: trodden sand and shingle, a few lupines and buckthorn against the walls. |
| beach | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord, lowmouth, ribshore, ribstrand |  |
| black_sand_beach | glassfall | glassfall, kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord |  |
| bloom | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, hemlow, hemlow_lanes, jadewater, kitehold, saltmirror, saurischia | Where the skystone changed what grows. Strongest at the crater, fading along the strewn field. |
| blue_hole | underwater | jadewater | The bottom of a blue hole: sand in the dark. |
| bone_down | windbarrow | windbarrow | The Bone Downs: a stretch of the Flint Down where it is mammoth bone that weathers out of the chalk and not flint. Skulls and ribs stand out of the rubble, ivory splinters lie about, and the diggers will not work it. |
| bottomland | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | The delta's ordinary ground: low wet woods of tupelo and oak along the old levees, palmettos under them, moss hanging everywhere. |
| boulder_fall | vermilion | vermilion | The broken slope under a mesa's cliffs where its caprock has fallen: red sand thick with sandstone boulders, juniper and sagebrush between them. |
| brine_edge | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The wet salt round the brine: glasswort in red mats and the flamingos standing in rows along the water. |
| brine_flat | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The brine at the low end of the pan: a few centimetres of rosy water over slick mud, where the flamingos feed. |
| burned_cane | lowmouth | lowmouth | A cane field burned off: black stubble and ash, a few charred stalks standing, and the woven stick lattices along the path through it. |
| camp_yard | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The camp: trodden sand over the gravel round the cabins, tamarisks planted for shade and gone wild, rabbitbrush coming up through the motor pool. |
| cane_field | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Sugar cane in blocks taller than a man, dirt rows between. |
| cane_town | lowmouth | lowmouth | Mire, the cane town: mown yards between the cottages, a few live oaks hung with moss, dirt lanes. |
| canopy_grove | - | no shipped world | Test bed for the canopy town: emergent kapoks and redwoods over strangler figs and a laurel canopy, lianas, ferns and moss. |
| canyon_floor | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The Sorrel's floodplain on the canyon floor: cottonwood and willow, tamarisk lower down. |
| cenote_reef | underwater | jadewater | Inside a deep cenote: coral in bands down the walls of the shaft, thickest in the sunlit top, fans and sponges lower down; vines and ferns hanging over the rim. |
| chalk_down | windbarrow | peregrine, windbarrow | The crowns and steeper flanks of the downs, grazed short: thin green-gold turf with the chalk coming through, thyme and juniper, no tall grass. |
| chenier | lowmouth | gannet, kaldvik, lowmouth, ribshore | The chenier: an old shell ridge on the south point, live oaks bent by the Gulf wind, palms, oyster shell underfoot, the tombs of the parish and the light at its tip. |
| cinder_field | redrock | kitehold, redrock, saurischia | Black cinders around the young cone; a few pines and nothing else. |
| city_commercial | city | no shipped world | Commercial and industrial land: little planted. |
| city_park | city | no shipped world | City parks: lawn under big scattered trees. |
| city_wood | city | no shipped world | Wooded slopes and green belts: fir and maple over ferns. |
| city_yard | city | no shipped world | Yards, verges and the green between houses: the default of a city world. |
| cold_coral | underwater | kaldvik | Deep fjord walls and the slopes under them: thickets of white Lophelia, red sea fans standing across the tide, cod hanging over it and jellyfish drifting past. |
| conifer_slopes | glassfall | glassfall |  |
| coral_dunes | redrock | redrock | A field of wind-blown coral-pink sand in the south-west: bare crests and slip faces, a few tough plants in the troughs, more toward the edges where the sand thins over the desert. |
| crash_burn | saltmirror | saltmirror | The burn round the wreck, ten years on: black snags of the old bushes standing over fynbos that came back thicker than anywhere else on the range, proteas crowding the scar, pieces of the hull lying in it all the way down the slope. |
| cypress_swamp | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | The back swamp: bald cypress and tupelo standing in black tea-coloured water, knees round their feet, moss in long beards, hyacinth on the still water, lily pads on the open water between the trunks. |
| delta_bed | lowmouth | lowmouth | The sound round the delta and the floor of every open water: soft grey mud under a metre or so of brown water, with rafts of lily pads drifting on the quieter reaches. |
| delta_shallows | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The channel beds: clean white sand under water a metre or two deep, clear enough to count the fish in it. The sand is why the flood runs clear by the time it is past the first islands. |
| drowned_parish | lowmouth | lowmouth | The bay over the old parish: open brown water a metre or two deep, a church tower and the tops of tombs and roofs standing out of it, lily pads over the drowned lots. |
| dunes | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, redrock, ribshore, ribstrand, saltmirror, saltmirror_array |  |
| edge_hanger | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The beech hanger on the face of Hemlow Edge and the combes: bare oolite between beeches clinging to the slope, ferns, vines over the lip. |
| eelgrass_flats | lowmouth | glassfall, jadewater | Drowned meadow: fields of eelgrass over the sheltered salt shallows inside a reef or a lagoon, growing under the water instead of stopping at the waterline, with turtles grazing it. Fresh water has its own: backwater_flats, which carries the lily pads. |
| eye_rim | saltmirror | saltmirror | Round the Turquoise and its eyes: wet salt banded orange, olive and white with the mats that live in the brine, glasswort in red patches, avocet-thin waders and the flamingos that come over from the Brine. |
| fae_commercial | faerie | no shipped world | Lots and yards round the bigger buildings, filling with trees. |
| fae_deck | - | no shipped world | The wood that has taken an arena: deep moss underfoot, bigleaf maple and giant fir with ferns and salal between the trunks. It dresses the open ground an arena is sunk into, not the arena itself: nothing a biome scatters on the ground reaches a room under it, so what grows inside comes from the materials painted on the structure. |
| fae_park | faerie | no shipped world | Parks gone to glades: great oaks on moss. |
| fae_wood | faerie | no shipped world | Green belts grown to deep wood. |
| fae_yard | faerie | no shipped world | The enchanted city's yards and verges: woodland coming back between the houses. |
| feathergrass_sea | windbarrow | peregrine, windbarrow | The open steppe: waist-high feathergrass gone gold over chalk, big tussocks standing out of it, a hawthorn or a juniper every few hundred metres. The default of Windbarrow. |
| flint_down | windbarrow | windbarrow | The high ground east of the Edge: short sparse grass over chalk rubble, more flint than soil, the diggers' pits and spoil, and the cave mouths under it. |
| flood_meadow | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | The flooded grass: short green sward on sand a hand or two under the water for two months of the year, standing in water you can see your feet through. The lechwe herds and everything that follows them. |
| floodplain | glassfall | glassfall |  |
| fore_reef | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The fore reef: spurs of coral running down to the drop-off, the richest water on the reef: staghorn thickets, tables, plates, brain and boulder coral, sponges and fans. |
| frost_hollow | windbarrow | windbarrow | A hollow in the downs where the grass is rimed white and glittering at any hour of a high-summer afternoon. Nothing is wrong with it but that; the sheep will not go down into it. |
| fw_apron | bonebight | bonebight | Rubble and silt at the foot of Frondwell's wall, with sponges and the odd anemone. |
| fw_crest | bonebight | bonebight | The outer lip of Frondwell's ring, where the swell breaks. |
| fw_flat | bonebight | bonebight | The crest and flat of Frondwell's ring, a metre or two under the sea: bull kelp in the surge, anemones and sea stars in the pools, mussels where it breaks the surface. |
| fw_islet | bonebight | bonebight | Frondwell's islets: black basalt with a cap of turf, salal and a few wind-bent shore pines and spruce, harbour seals hauled out on the rocks. |
| fw_kelp | bonebight | bonebight | Frondwell's kelp forest on the outer slope of the ring: giant kelp rising from the basalt to a canopy on the surface, rockfish and kelp perch among the stipes. |
| fw_kelp_groove | bonebight | bonebight | Sand grooves between the kelp-covered spurs of Frondwell's outer slope. |
| fw_lagoon | bonebight | bonebight | Frondwell's lagoon: fifteen metres of sheltered water over pale sand, kelp on the rocky patches, eelgrass on the sand. |
| fw_pale | bonebight | bonebight | Where the pale from the black glass under Station Calypso has taken the kelp: glass growths like coral on the rock, pale kelp, the fish few and slow. |
| fw_pale_islet | bonebight | bonebight | Frondwell's islet nearest Calypso, gone pale: spiral pines, bleached turf, glass pushing up through it. |
| fw_patch | bonebight | bonebight | Rocky knolls in Frondwell's lagoon, crowned with kelp. |
| fw_wall | bonebight | bonebight | The drop-off outside Frondwell's ring: basalt falling to thirty metres, white plumose anemones and cloud sponges on every ledge, the kelp canopy far overhead. |
| fynbos | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The fynbos on the range: a low grey-green fleece of heath and restio reed with proteas standing out of it, pincushions in the open and rock showing through everywhere. |
| fynbos_kloof | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The ravines in the range: deeper soil and the little water there is, silver trees standing in it and the fynbos taller round them. |
| gallery_forest | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Gallery forest: closed high forest along the shaded bank of the upper river, figs rooted into the scarp, vines, ferns, and no view out of it at all. |
| gh_barrens | gannet | gannet | The low barrens behind the beach south of the bay: lowbush blueberry carpeting sandy ground, crimson in autumn, with heather and a bent pine or two. |
| gh_bay_floor | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold | The floor of Mercy Bay: sand under eelgrass, sheltered and shallow. |
| gh_clam_flats | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold | Clam flats in the inner bay: sand and mud covered at high water and bare at low, with mussel beds on the harder ground. |
| gh_faewood | gannet | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The north-east corner, where the redwoods give way to a wood that is plainly somebody else's: the edge of the faerie wood north of here. |
| gh_grotto | gannet | gannet | Hogback over the grotto: the island's turf above, and below it the cave pool, lit blue by the sea coming in under the rock, with reef fish round its coral. |
| gh_hayfield | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold | A hayfield behind the walls, the grass left long and cut in strips late in the year. |
| gh_island | gannet | gannet | Hake and Hogback islands above the shore: granite, wind-cut turf, bent shore pines, heather and lichen. |
| gh_orchard | gannet | gannet, hemlow, kitehold | An orchard by a farmhouse: apple trees in loose rows in short grass, red apples on the crowns in autumn. |
| gh_pasture | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold | Gannet Harbor's sheep pasture inside dry-stone walls: turf grazed short, a thorn left for shade, an oak or maple to a field. |
| gh_puffinry | gannet | gannet | The puffin colony on Hake Island's west cliff tops: turf slopes riddled with burrows, greener round them for the guano. |
| gh_redwood | gannet | gannet, hemlow | Coast redwood along the north edge: tall trunks with young redwoods coming up round them, fern and huckleberry under, fog in the crowns. |
| gh_redwood_grove | gannet | gannet, hemlow | The old groves in the redwoods: the giants alone, bare trunks over a floor of fern, nothing else growing in their shade. |
| gh_river | gannet | gannet | The Cold River's banks: rounded rock and gravel, sword fern and moss, alder and bigleaf maple leaning over the water. |
| gh_salt_marsh | gannet | gannet, kitehold | Salt marsh at the calm south-east corner of Mercy Bay: cordgrass and glasswort on mud the tide covers. |
| gh_spruce | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor, kitehold | Ribshore's Sitka spruce carried on along Gannet Harbor's east edge, where the bay brings the coast too near for the spruce rule to hold. |
| gh_town | gannet | gannet, gannet_harbor | The town's lawns and verges: mown grass, street trees, roses by the doors. Kept, with nobody to keep it. |
| ghost_forest | lowmouth | lowmouth | The ghost forest on the bay's north shore: grey dead cypress standing in the water where the salt came in, cormorants and anhingas on every snag, lily pads between the dead trunks. |
| glass_reef | underwater | glassfall | Glassfall's strange reef, nearest the strewn field: staghorn, tables and fans of pale glass, faintly lit, among the ordinary coral. |
| glow_cenote | jadewater | jadewater | The rims and walls of the flooded sinkholes: moss lit by the film beneath, vine curtains, banyans on the walls, kite rays rising. |
| golden_savanna | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | Open savanna: gold tussock grass on rolling sand, flat-topped acacias in loose stands, baobabs standing alone a hundred metres apart, and termite mounds. The country between the water and the pans. |
| gravel_fan | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The gravel fans at the foot of the range: sand washed out of the ravines over grey gravel, dry grass tufts on it, and the cactus forest on them, cardon and saguaro over ocotillo, cholla, prickly pear and agave, with lizards everywhere. |
| green_kurgan | windbarrow | windbarrow | The one mound in the field that is green when the rest are gold, and the ring of ground round it: thick wet-looking turf, and no grazing marks on it. |
| grimwold | - | hemlow, saurischia | The Grimwold, on the high ground over Raven Scar: the edge of an older world. Monkey-puzzles standing high over a thicket of podocarp scrub, seed ferns, tree ferns and cycads, ginkgos among them, horsetails along the Wend, yews thick along the top of the Scar, moss and fern underfoot and mist between the trunks. The village keeps away from it. |
| gulf_beach | lowmouth | lowmouth | The Gulf shore of the chenier: oyster shell and pale sand, wrack along the tide line. |
| hammock | lowmouth | gannet, jadewater, kaldvik, lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou, ribshore, thornpan, thornpan_delta | A hammock: a tree island in the sawgrass, a little higher and dry, thick with cabbage palm, oak, strangler fig and palmetto. |
| harlow | redrock | gannet, glassfall, hemlow, redrock, saltmirror | The ground around Harlow, trampled and dug: rabbitbrush and the town's leftovers. |
| hem_banks | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The Hem's banks: willows and alders, reedmace in the slack water, kingfishers, herons and dragonflies. |
| hem_heath | - | hemlow | The heath on the rising ground in the north-east, between the north road and the Grimwold: gorse, heather and bracken, a few pines and birches, larks overhead. |
| hemlow_corn | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The village's barley, in walled fields on the better ground: pale ears to the knee, nothing else growing in it. |
| hemlow_green | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The village on the top of the Knap: lawns, cottage-garden roses and cherries, the great oak on the green. |
| hemlow_meadow | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | The hay meadows and pasture on the Knap and down the valley: flower-thick turf, a few great oaks and elders, dry-stone walls, fireflies at dusk. |
| hemlow_pasture | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | Sheep pasture inside the village's dry-stone walls: turf grazed short, a thorn or two left for shade, one old oak to a field. |
| high_meadow | redrock | redrock | Grass parks on the plateau where the soil stays wet in spring: gold grass, aspen and young pines at the edges. |
| hob_clearing | - | hemlow | The clearing round the Hob Stones: short grass where no tree grows, a ring of glassbloom round it, sky rays turning overhead. |
| hollins | - | gannet, hemlow, kitehold, saurischia | The Hollins: old oak and beech wood with hazel and fern beneath, turning strange near the Hob Stones (gold maples, cherry trees, blossom vines, pale birch, glassbloom). The default of Hemlow. |
| hollins_beech | - | hemlow, hemlow_lanes | Beechwood on the high ground of the Hollins: tall grey trunks close together, a floor of brown leaves with almost nothing growing on it, fern only where a tree has come down. |
| hollins_deep | - | hemlow | The heart of the Hollins, where the wood is plainly somebody else's: oaks a hundred feet high and as far round as a cottage, gold maples and wisteria grown into trees between them, blossom hanging from everything, glassbloom at their feet, and white deer with one horn among the others. |
| hollins_dell | - | hemlow | The wet hollows of the Hollins: alder, willow and birch over fern to the waist, every trunk green, mist lying in it by evening and lights in the mist. |
| hollins_glade | - | hemlow | A glade in the Hollins: a ring of open grass and flowers the trees stop at, with nothing anyone can point to that keeps them out. |
| ice_deck | transmission | porch, transmission | The snowfield a frozen base is dug into: wind-packed snow and nothing else. It dresses the plain over a buried arena, not the arena itself; what is inside comes from the materials the ice style paints on the structure. |
| jade_shallows | jadewater | jadewater | The lagoon floor: white lime sand under one to four metres of green water, turquoise from above. |
| jungle_temple | jadewater | jadewater | The Order's temple grounds at the foot of the Old Wall: moss over everything, banyans in the walls, stone lanterns and rows of little statues. |
| kaldvik_fell | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | The high fell above the fjord walls: wind-packed snow over basalt, a few junipers hunched in the lee of boulders. |
| kaldvik_fjord | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | The fjord floor: cold dark water, herring in winter shoals and what follows them, the humpbacks (one in seven of them white here) and a pod of orcas. |
| kaldvik_green | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | The hamlet's cleared ground: trodden snow between the houses, a few birches and rowans kept for shelter, nothing to stop a sledge. |
| kaldvik_shore | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | A fjord's narrow shore: dark shingle under a rind of snow, kelp wrack and ice at the tideline. |
| kaldvik_spruce | kaldvik | kaldvik, kaldvik_fjord | Snow-bound spruce on the lower walls and the valley floors of a northern fjord: dense dark firs, a few bare birches, deep snow between. |
| karst_jungle | jadewater | jadewater | The lowland between the Old Wall and the lagoon: banyans over oak and alder, ferns, vines on everything. |
| karst_pinnacle | jadewater | jadewater | The tops and faces of the towers and the Old Wall: thin turf, wind-twisted pines, vine curtains over every lip, banyans out of the cliffs. Koi in the Order's rain pools on the tower tops. |
| kelp_forest | glassfall | bonebight, gannet, glassfall, kaldvik, ribshore, ribstrand | Giant kelp on rocky reef 3 to 16 m down along the coast: stipes to the surface, a floating canopy, herring and the odd manta among them. |
| kh_bog | kitehold | kitehold | Cushion bog along the moor's streams and round the tarns: a wet sphagnum lawn, rusty in places, heaped with hard bright-green cushion domes grown into one another, frailejones and tussocks standing in it. |
| kh_cliff | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | Bare basalt on the faces of the Kite, the Steps and the gorge: columns, moss in the shade, ferns on the ledges. |
| kh_cloud | kitehold | kitehold | Cloud forest on the upper bench and the back face of the Steps, inside the cloud most afternoons: tree ferns, podocarps and laurels dripping with moss, bromeliads up every trunk. |
| kh_cloud_high | kitehold | kitehold | Cloud forest on the foot of the high moor, above the back face of the Steps where the cloud sits. |
| kh_cottage_garden | kitehold | kitehold | The cottage gardens along the holloway north of Maplebridge: dug beds, sunflowers and lupins against the fence, a rose by the door, an apple tree. |
| kh_dogtown | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A prairie-dog town: the grass bitten to the roots over a patch a hundred metres or so across, fifty to seventy-five burrow mounds on pale aprons of kicked-out soil, sentries sitting up on them. |
| kh_fernwood | kitehold | kitehold | Saurischia's Fernwood where it comes over the edge and thins into the Plain: the same high forest, broken into clumps and glades. |
| kh_flax | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A field of flax for Kitehold's linen and kite cloth: sky-blue in flower in early summer. |
| kh_hamlet | kitehold | kitehold | Maplebridge's yards and verges among the maples: grass, wood piles and big old maples left standing. |
| kh_kipuka | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A kipuka: an island of old ground the lava went round, older trees and wildflowers standing in black rock. |
| kh_maple | kitehold | kitehold | The maple woods east of the Lessa: sugar maples gone crimson and amber, a few big-leaf maples and birch, ferns and trillium under them. |
| kh_marsh | kitehold | bonebight, kitehold | The marsh at the foot of the farms where the Lessa slows: reed and cattail beds, open pools with lilies, willows on the hummocks. |
| kh_paramo | kitehold | kitehold | The páramo, the high wet moor over the cloud: straw tussock grass, frailejones in their thousands (most knee to chest high, the old ones on tall shaggy trunks), gorse in flower, bright cushion bog in the hollows, lichened tors, fog half the year. |
| kh_plain | kitehold | bonebight, kitehold, kitehold_rock | The Plain: tall gold bunchgrass on ash and loess, feathergrass tussocks, a wind-bent thorn here and there, wild horses and bison. |
| kh_redwood | kitehold | kitehold | The redwoods on the first bench of the Steps, in the wet under the cloud: tall red columns, sorrel and fern, moss up the north side. |
| kh_ribbon | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A buried stream bed under the Plain: the same tan grass as round it, but with lupine through about two tufts in five, a wandering band of violet in the gold. kh_ribbon_red and kh_ribbon_orange are the same with paintbrush and poppies. |
| kh_ribbon_orange | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A buried stream bed under the Plain with poppy through the tan grass. |
| kh_ribbon_red | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A buried stream bed under the Plain with paintbrush through the tan grass. |
| kh_riverside | kitehold | kitehold | The banks of the Lessa and its becks: willow, alder and osier, ferns and mossy boulders. Black bears come down to fish the salmon. |
| kh_salt_marsh | kitehold | kitehold | Salt marsh where the wet meadows meet the estuary: cordgrass and glasswort cut by tidal creeks. |
| kh_sunflower | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | A field of sunflowers grown for oil, head high in late summer. |
| kh_titans | kitehold | kitehold | The Hollow Titans' grove: the biggest redwoods on the Steps, round the ring of trunks the road goes under. |
| kh_town | kitehold | kitehold, kitehold_rock | Kitehold's streets and yards on top of the Kite: beaten earth and worn basalt, a juniper or a thorn in a yard, sparrows and crows. |
| kh_vale_meadow | kitehold | kitehold | The Lessa's valley floor north of Maplebridge: hay meadow and grazing down to the water, big old maples standing alone in the grass, hawthorn and apple by the lane, the maple wood starting where the slopes do. |
| kh_wet_meadow | kitehold | kitehold | Wet meadow at the foot of the hills above the estuary: tussock and rush, alder and willow along the drains, cranes and geese grazing. |
| kopje_rock | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | A kopje: bare granite dome and the boulders split off it, lichen in the shade, a rock fig rooted in a crack, and lions asleep on the warm stone. |
| lagoon | underwater | jadewater | An atoll's lagoon: pale sand under clear shallow water, here and there a brain coral. |
| lake_bed | redrock | redrock, vermilion | Mud under the reservoir. |
| lake_shore | redrock | redrock | The ground the falling lake left: cracked mud and sand, tamarisk taking hold. |
| lantern_dell | - | hemlow | Lantern Dell: the floor and walls of the sinkhole behind Lantern Spout, open to the sky. Moss that glows faintly, fern from every ledge, vines down the walls, pale birches and glassbloom on the floor. |
| lantern_spout | - | hemlow | Round Lantern Spout and its pool: moss lit faintly by the glow-worms, ferns in the spray, vines down the cliff. |
| levee | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | The levee and the high ground along the Marrow: mown grass, a few great live oaks, cottonwoods and willows down the bank. |
| lime_beach | jadewater | jadewater | White lime-sand beaches at the foot of the jungle and round the towers. |
| lowmouth_village | lowmouth | lowmouth, lowmouth_bayou | Lowmouth, the stilt village on the old channel: houses and camps on posts, boardwalks and footbridges over the water, boats tied under them. |
| mangrove_keys | lowmouth | jadewater, lowmouth | The new mouth: red mangroves on their prop roots in the shallows, black mangroves on the mud keys, channels winding between, the river's silt settling. |
| mesa_pines | vermilion | vermilion | The ponderosa forest on top of Vermilion Mesa: Redrock's plateau forest, placed by region instead of by height, since the mesa top is far lower than Redrock's plateau. |
| mirror_salt | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The Mirror: the salt pan, flat to the horizon, a crust of plates over grey mud with nothing growing on it. Pronghorn cross it in the mornings; the vultures and ravens work it from above. |
| mixed_forest | glassfall | glassfall |  |
| motu | underwater | jadewater | A motu: a low islet of coral sand on an atoll's ring, a few palms and scrub on it. |
| mound_grounds | lowmouth | lowmouth | The mound grounds: short grass kept down by deer on the great bird mound and the low half rings east of it, a few old oaks. |
| mudflat | lowmouth | gannet, gannet_harbor, glassfall, jadewater, kitehold, lowmouth, redrock | Mud where the river's silt comes out at the mouth's edge: a few wet bars, drift logs and dead snags, wading birds walking it, mist lying on it at dawn and dusk. |
| narrow_banks | windbarrow | windbarrow | The green line of the Narrow: willow and alder leaning downwind, reedmace in the slack water, long meadow grass, and everything in the country coming down to drink. |
| oak_ground | windbarrow | windbarrow | The few metres round the great oak below Durn: the same turf as the village keeps, and one tree in it. A small region plus `lone` spacing wider than the region is how a world plants exactly one tree. |
| painted_badlands | redrock | redrock | Banded purple and grey clay hills with petrified logs weathering out; almost nothing grows. |
| palm_island | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | A palm island in the delta: a sand knoll a metre and a half up, ringed with fan palms grown from nuts that went through an elephant, with grass on its crown and usually one baobab. |
| pan_floor | thornpan | thornpan, thornpan_delta | A salt pan: the floor of a lake that has not existed for fourteen thousand years, swept bare and cracked into plates, with dead baobabs on its rim and shells lying on it in drifts. |
| patch_reef | underwater | jadewater | A patch reef standing up out of the lagoon sand to a metre or two of the surface: a knot of staghorn, tables and brain coral. |
| pinyon_juniper | redrock | redrock | The bench between the Red and White Cliffs: pinyon and juniper woodland over sage. |
| ponderosa_forest | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The plateau: open park-like ponderosa pine forest on needle duff, gambel oak beneath, aspen in the damp hollows. |
| prairie | glassfall | glassfall, redrock | What a forest becomes for a lifetime after fire. |
| pucklands | - | hemlow | The Pucklands, where Chepworth Down turns strange: turf very short and very green, rings of toadstools tens of metres across that the sheep won't graze inside, a lone thorn or two, glassbloom round Puck's Barrow, pale hares at dusk and fae horses after dark. |
| red_desert | redrock | redrock, vermilion | The open sandy bench south of the Red Cliffs: sagebrush, scattered juniper, yucca, boulders. |
| reef_apron | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | Rubble and sand at the wall's foot, giving way to the old sea floor: a few boulders and barrels. |
| reef_crest | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The crest, where the swell breaks: elkhorn thickets leaning into it. |
| reef_flat | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The reef flat behind the crest, a metre or so under: low brain and finger coral on pale rubble, sand between. |
| reef_groove | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The sand channels between the spurs: white sand rippled by the surge, a brain coral here and there. |
| reef_wall | underwater | glassfall, jadewater | The drop-off: a near-sheer wall of reef limestone falling into the blue, fans and sponges standing out from it, plate coral shelving its face. |
| rib_beach | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, gannet_harbor, ribshore, ribstrand | The wide flat beach: wet grey-brown sand that holds the sky, as far as you can see each way. |
| rib_blowout | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Bare sand on the dunes' steep lee faces and blown-out crests, where nothing holds. |
| rib_boneyard | ribshore | ribshore | The sea floor round the titan's skull and jaw: silt with bone grit in it, mats of bacteria, white anemones on everything that stands. |
| rib_dunes | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | The old dunes, held by grass: tan dunegrass combed by the wind, purple lupine in drifts and thickets of sea buckthorn in the hollows. |
| rib_headland | ribshore | ribshore | The basalt headland: wind-cut turf and heather, shore pines bent flat, the cliffs white with birds. |
| rib_hollow | ribshore | ribshore | Blowouts: bowls the wind has dug down through the dunes to the damp sand, with water standing in the bottom after rain, a snag or two, elk tracks. |
| rib_kelp | ribshore | gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Bull kelp over the rock reefs off the headland: a canopy at the surface, columns of light under it. Since v2: lion's mane jellies, wolf eels, opal squid and frond dragons from Bonebight's Frondwell. |
| rib_mat | ribshore | ribshore | Mats of white and orange bacteria on the silt closest to the bone. |
| rib_oldgrowth | ribshore | gannet | The old stand inland: Douglas fir and redwood over sword fern and salal, the trunks too wide to reach round, a few fallen giants. |
| rib_plain | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | The flat behind the beach and between the dune fields: dunegrass and lupine on old sand, wet in the hollows. |
| rib_shelf | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | The open sea floor: sand ribbed by the swell, grey under green water. |
| rib_shoals | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | Shallow water over sand off the beach, eelgrass in the hollows. |
| rib_spruce | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore | The strip of Sitka spruce behind the dunes: dark, wet, mossy, fern underneath. |
| rib_stumps | ribshore | ribshore | A spruce forest the sea drowned: tall silver poles standing in the water and the wet sand, stumps between them. |
| rib_tidepools | ribshore | ribshore | Rock shelves at the waterline under the headland, cut into pools: mussels, sea stars, green anemones, sea palms where the surf hits. |
| rib_wrack | ribshore | bonebight, gannet, ribshore, ribstrand | The wrack line at the dune foot: dry sand, driftwood thrown up by the storms, and the last of the dunegrass. |
| rib_wrack_giants | bonebight | bonebight, gannet, ribshore | Ribshore's wrack line with the giant driftwood of the northern coast heaped in it: whole storm-thrown cedar and spruce trunks two and three metres thick, root plates and log walls among the ordinary drift. For the open coasts, not the kart circuits. |
| river_oasis | redrock | redrock | A sandbar standing out of a desert lake or river, watered from underneath: palms, figs and reeds in a green tuft, and the animals that swam out to it. |
| rock_island | saltmirror | saltmirror, saltmirror_array | The rock islands: low knolls of grey quartzite standing out of the salt, the only dry rock on the pan, crowded with cardon and saguaro and ocotillo, and lizards on every warm face. |
| salt_bed | windbarrow | windbarrow | The bed of Saltmere: grey salt mud under a metre or two of still, milky water. |
| salt_shore | windbarrow | thornpan, thornpan_delta, windbarrow | Round Saltmere: a white crust of salt in plates over grey mud, low grey scrub back from it, and nothing else growing. |
| sau_ash | saurischia | saurischia | The ash country north-east of Cinderhorn: grey ash from the last eruption, dead snags of the forest it buried, horsetails and ferns coming back in the gullies. |
| sau_brake | saurischia | saurischia | Tree-fern brakes and horsetail beds along the streams of the Fernwood, under dawn redwoods, with eelgrass in the slow water, trout and pond turtles in it and gulls come up from the mere. |
| sau_cinder | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | Cinderhorn's cone of black cinders: a few pines, and fireweed come back in magenta drifts on the cinders three hundred years after the last eruption. |
| sau_city | saurischia | saurischia | Cascadelle's streets, quays and squares: pale limestone flags, plane trees along the boulevards, compsognathus underfoot. |
| sau_faewood | saurischia | saurischia | The south-west corner, where the faerie wood in Hemlow's south-east corner comes over the edge: the same somebody-else's wood, with toadstool rings in its clearings. |
| sau_ferncross | saurischia | saurischia | The ground of Ferncross, cleared round the stilt houses: trodden fern floor, a few monkey-puzzles left standing. |
| sau_fernwood | saurischia | kitehold, saurischia | The Fernwood, the high forest that fills the middle of Saurischia: monkey-puzzles standing over ginkgos and podocarps, tree ferns and cycads in the gaps, ferns underfoot. Raptors hunt it and stegosaurs browse its edges. |
| sau_fields | saurischia | saurischia | Cascadelle's grain fields: wheat and barley in hedged plots. |
| sau_garden | saurischia | saurischia | Gardens and green squares in Cascadelle: lawns, planes and flowering cherries, clumps of cycads. |
| sau_glade | saurischia | saurischia | A fern glade in the Fernwood: an opening of waist-high ferns and cycads with the forest standing round it. |
| sau_highwood | saurischia | saurischia | The open forest on the high country over the Steps: monkey-puzzles and firs further apart than in the Fernwood, with grass between. |
| sau_lava | saurischia | saurischia | Cinderhorn's young lava running south off the cone: bare broken black crust, nothing growing yet. |
| sau_marsh | saurischia | saurischia | The horsetail marsh where the Wend slows on its way west: giant horsetails, reeds and cattails over wet ground. |
| sau_orchard | saurischia | saurischia | Orchards round the farms: apples in rows over mown grass. |
| sau_parkland | saurischia | saurischia | The Parkland: long grass with monkey-puzzles standing alone and ginkgos in small groves, cycads in clumps; the long-necks browse it. |
| sau_pasture | saurischia | saurischia | Pasture and hay round the farms and Spray Meadow, with hawthorn along the edges and the odd ginkgo. |
